const LightBasis=Object.freeze({
  fromDirection(direction){
    const n=RayMath.normalize(direction);
    const helper=Math.abs(n[2])<.999?[0,0,1]:[0,1,0];
    const tangent=RayMath.normalize(RayMath.cross(helper,n));
    const bitangent=RayMath.cross(n,tangent);
    return {n,tangent,bitangent};
  },
  pointOnRectangle(light,u,v){
    const b=this.fromDirection(light.direction);
    const du=(u-.5)*light.width,dv=(v-.5)*light.height;
    return RayMath.add(light.position,RayMath.add(RayMath.mul(b.tangent,du),RayMath.mul(b.bitangent,dv)));
  },
  pointOnDisc(light,u,v){
    const b=this.fromDirection(light.direction);
    const d=RenderSampling.concentricDisk(u,v);
    const r=light.size*.5;
    return RayMath.add(light.position,RayMath.add(RayMath.mul(b.tangent,d[0]*r),RayMath.mul(b.bitangent,d[1]*r)));
  },
  pointOnSphere(light,u,v){
    const z=1-2*u;
    const r=Math.sqrt(Math.max(0,1-z*z));
    const phi=2*Math.PI*v;
    const local=[r*Math.cos(phi),r*Math.sin(phi),z];
    return RayMath.add(light.position,RayMath.mul(local,light.size*.5));
  }
});

const EnvironmentSampler=Object.freeze({
  findDome(lights){return (lights||[]).find(l=>l.enabled!==false&&l.type==='dome')||null;},
  radiance(light,direction){
    if(!light||light.enabled===false||light.type!=='dome')return [0,0,0];
    return LightEnergyPolicy.radiance(light);
  },
  background(renderScene,settings,lights,rayDirection){
    const dome=this.findDome(lights);
    if(dome)return this.radiance(dome,rayDirection);
    return RenderColor.hexToLinear(settings.background||'#202020');
  }
});

const ShadowVisibility=Object.freeze({
  visible(acceleration,origin,direction,maxDistance){
    try{LiteTraceExecutionContext.performance?.addRay?.(RenderRayType.SHADOW);}catch(_){}
    const eps=RenderRayPolicy.epsilonForPoint(origin);
    if(Number.isFinite(maxDistance)&&maxDistance<=eps*2)return true;
    const o=RayMath.add(origin,RayMath.mul(direction,eps));
    const limit=Number.isFinite(maxDistance)?Math.max(eps,maxDistance-eps):Infinity;
    const ray=new RenderRay(o,direction,eps,limit,RenderRayType.SHADOW,0);
    return !acceleration.occluded(ray);
  }
});

const AreaLightSampler=Object.freeze({
  sample(light,point,u=.5,v=.5){
    if(light.type==='rectangle'){
      const lp=LightBasis.pointOnRectangle(light,u,v);
      return this._local(light,point,lp,RenderLightPolicy.area(light),light.direction);
    }
    if(light.type==='disc'){
      const lp=LightBasis.pointOnDisc(light,u,v);
      return this._local(light,point,lp,RenderLightPolicy.area(light),light.direction);
    }
    if(light.type==='sphere'){
      const lp=LightBasis.pointOnSphere(light,u,v);
      const normal=RayMath.normalize(RayMath.sub(lp,light.position));
      return this._local(light,point,lp,RenderLightPolicy.area(light),normal);
    }
    if(light.type==='mesh'){
      // Part 4 keeps Mesh Light as a rectangular proxy.
      // True emissive mesh-triangle sampling remains Group 10.
      const lp=LightBasis.pointOnRectangle(light,u,v);
      return this._local(light,point,lp,RenderLightPolicy.area(light),light.direction);
    }
    return DirectLightSampler.sample(light,point);
  },

  _local(light,point,lightPoint,area,emitterNormal=null){
    const to=RayMath.sub(lightPoint,point);
    const dist=Math.max(RayMath.length(to),1e-6);
    const dir=RayMath.mul(to,1/dist);

    if(light.range>0&&dist>=light.range){
      return {kind:'local',direction:dir,distance:dist,radiance:[0,0,0],lightPoint,area};
    }

    let attenuation=LightEnergyPolicy.distanceAttenuation(light,dist);

    let emitterCos=1;
    if(emitterNormal){
      emitterCos=Math.max(0,RayMath.dot(RayMath.normalize(emitterNormal),RayMath.mul(dir,-1)));
      if(emitterCos<=0){
        return {kind:'local',direction:dir,distance:dist,radiance:[0,0,0],lightPoint,area,emitterCos:0};
      }
    }

    const safeArea=Math.max(RenderLightPolicy.minArea,area);
    return {
      kind:'local',
      direction:dir,
      distance:dist,
      // Size changes sampling footprint/softness only; intensity remains the same user-facing unit.
      radiance:LightEnergyPolicy.radiance(light,attenuation*emitterCos),
      lightPoint,
      area:safeArea,
      emitterCos
    };
  }
});

var DirectLightingIntegrator=Object.freeze({
  samplesFor(light,settings){
    if(['rectangle','disc','sphere','mesh'].includes(light.type)){
      const preset=String(settings?.quality||'Preview');
      if(preset==='Ultra')return 16;
      if(preset==='High')return 8;
      if(preset==='Draft')return 1;
      return 4;
    }
    return 1;
  },

  evaluate(job,acceleration,compiled,ray,hit,pixelSeed=1,options={}){
    const material=RenderMaterialLibrary.resolveHit(job.renderScene,compiled.materials,hit);
    const n=SurfaceInterpolator.normal(acceleration,hit);
    const v=RayMath.mul(ray.direction,-1);
    const includeEmission=options.includeEmission!==false;
    const includeDome=options.includeDome!==false;
    let color=includeEmission?RenderColor.mul(material.emissive,material.emissiveIntensity):[0,0,0];

    if(material.shading==='unlit'){
      return includeEmission?RenderColor.add(material.baseColor,color):[0,0,0];
    }

    const emissiveSet=compiled.emissiveTriangles||job.emissiveTriangles||null;
    if(emissiveSet?.triangles?.length){
      const es=EmissiveTriangleSet.sample(emissiveSet,pixelSeed,0,hit.position);
      if(es&&ShadowVisibility.visible(acceleration,hit.position,es.direction,es.distance)){
        const brdf=PhysicalBSDF.evaluate(material,n,v,es.direction);
        const bsdfPdf=PathBSDFSampler.pdf(material,n,v,es.direction);
        const w=RenderMIS.power(es.pdf,bsdfPdf);
        color=RenderColor.add(color,RenderColor.mul(RenderColor.mul(brdf,es.radiance),w));
      }
    }

    for(let li=0;li<compiled.lights.length;li++){
      const light=compiled.lights[li];

      if(light.type==='dome'){
        if(includeDome){
          const ambient=EnvironmentSampler.radiance(light,n);
          const diffuse=RenderColor.mul(material.baseColor,(1-material.metalness)/Math.PI);
          color=RenderColor.add(color,RenderColor.mul(RenderColor.mul(diffuse,ambient),Math.PI));
        }
        continue;
      }

      const count=this.samplesFor(light,job.settings);
      let accum=[0,0,0];

      for(let si=0;si<count;si++){
        const uv=RenderSampling.sample2D(pixelSeed^(li*2654435761),si);
        const sample=['rectangle','disc','sphere','mesh'].includes(light.type)
          ? AreaLightSampler.sample(light,hit.position,uv[0],uv[1])
          : DirectLightSampler.sample(light,hit.position);

        if(!sample||!sample.direction)continue;
        if(!sample.radiance||sample.radiance.every(v=>v<=0||!Number.isFinite(v)))continue;

        if(light.castShadows!==false){

      }
    }

    const safeArea=Math.max(RenderLightPolicy.minArea,area);
    return {
      kind:'local',
      direction:dir,
      distance:dist,
      // Size changes sampling footprint/softness only; intensity remains the same user-facing unit.
      radiance:LightEnergyPolicy.radiance(light,attenuation*emitterCos),
      lightPoint,
      area:safeArea,
      emitterCos
    };
  }
});

var DirectLightingIntegrator=Object.freeze({
  samplesFor(light,settings){
    if(['rectangle','disc','sphere','mesh'].includes(light.type)){
      const preset=String(settings?.quality||'Preview');
      if(preset==='Ultra')return 16;
      if(preset==='High')return 8;
      if(preset==='Draft')return 1;
      return 4;
    }
    return 1;
  },

  evaluate(job,acceleration,compiled,ray,hit,pixelSeed=1,options={}){
    const material=RenderMaterialLibrary.resolveHit(job.renderScene,compiled.materials,hit);
    const n=SurfaceInterpolator.normal(acceleration,hit);
    const v=RayMath.mul(ray.direction,-1);
    const includeEmission=options.includeEmission!==false;
    const includeDome=options.includeDome!==false;
    let color=includeEmission?RenderColor.mul(material.emissive,material.emissiveIntensity):[0,0,0];

    if(material.shading==='unlit'){
      return includeEmission?RenderColor.add(material.baseColor,color):[0,0,0];
    }

    const emissiveSet=compiled.emissiveTriangles||job.emissiveTriangles||null;
    if(emissiveSet?.triangles?.length){
      const es=EmissiveTriangleSet.sample(emissiveSet,pixelSeed,0,hit.position);
      if(es&&ShadowVisibility.visible(acceleration,hit.position,es.direction,es.distance)){
        const brdf=PhysicalBSDF.evaluate(material,n,v,es.direction);
        const bsdfPdf=PathBSDFSampler.pdf(material,n,v,es.direction);
        const w=RenderMIS.power(es.pdf,bsdfPdf);
        color=RenderColor.add(color,RenderColor.mul(RenderColor.mul(brdf,es.radiance),w));
      }
    }

    for(let li=0;li<compiled.lights.length;li++){
      const light=compiled.lights[li];

      if(light.type==='dome'){
        if(includeDome){
          const ambient=EnvironmentSampler.radiance(light,n);
          const diffuse=RenderColor.mul(material.baseColor,(1-material.metalness)/Math.PI);
          color=RenderColor.add(color,RenderColor.mul(RenderColor.mul(diffuse,ambient),Math.PI));
        }
        continue;
      }

      const count=this.samplesFor(light,job.settings);
      let accum=[0,0,0];

      for(let si=0;si<count;si++){
        const uv=RenderSampling.sample2D(pixelSeed^(li*2654435761),si);
        const sample=['rectangle','disc','sphere','mesh'].includes(light.type)
          ? AreaLightSampler.sample(light,hit.position,uv[0],uv[1])
          : DirectLightSampler.sample(light,hit.position);

        if(!sample||!sample.direction)continue;
        if(!sample.radiance||sample.radiance.every(v=>v<=0||!Number.isFinite(v)))continue;

        if(light.castShadows!==false){
          const visible=ShadowVisibility.visible(acceleration,hit.position,sample.direction,sample.distance);
          if(!visible)continue;
        }

        const brdf=PhysicalBSDF.evaluate(material,n,v,sample.direction);
        accum=RenderColor.add(accum,RenderColor.mul(brdf,sample.radiance));
      }

      if(count>1)accum=RenderColor.mul(accum,1/count);
      color=RenderColor.add(color,accum);
    }

    return color;
  }
});

var RenderCoreD=Object.freeze({
  version:'3.90.0-core-d',

  evaluateSurfaceDirect(job,acceleration,compiled,ray,hit,pixelSeed,options={}){
    return DirectLightingIntegrator.evaluate(
      job,acceleration,compiled,ray,hit,pixelSeed,options
    );
  },

  evaluateEnvironment(job,compiled,rayDirection){
    return EnvironmentSampler.background(
      job.renderScene,job.settings,compiled.lights,rayDirection
    );
  },

  tracePath(job,acceleration,compiled,ray,pixelSeed,initialHit=null){
    return PathIntegrator.trace(
      job,acceleration,compiled,ray,pixelSeed,initialHit
    );
  },

  visible(acceleration,origin,direction,maxDistance){
    return ShadowVisibility.visible(acceleration,origin,direction,maxDistance);
  },

  validate(){
    const errors=[];
    const g7=validateRenderGroup7();if(!g7.ok)errors.push(...g7.errors);
    const g8=validateRenderGroup8();if(!g8.ok)errors.push(...g8.errors);
    if(typeof this.evaluateSurfaceDirect!=='function')errors.push('Core D direct evaluator missing');
    if(typeof this.tracePath!=='function')errors.push('Core D path tracer missing');
    if(typeof this.evaluateEnvironment!=='function')errors.push('Core D environment evaluator missing');
    return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
  }
});
window.RenderCoreD=RenderCoreD;

    'shadow-ray visibility',

    'constant-color dome environment radiance',
    'direct-light sample accumulation'
  ]),
  consumes:Object.freeze([
    'Core B acceleration trace',
    'Core C materials and scene lights',
    'Group 5 PhysicalBSDF'
  ]),
  doesNotOwn:Object.freeze([
    'path GI',
    'MIS',
    'adaptive sampling',
    'HDRI importance sampling',
    'light cache',
    'path guiding'
  ])
});

function runRenderGroup7SelfTests(){
  const errors=[];

  {
    const store=new RenderTriangleStore();
    store.addPacket({
      objectId:'shadow-test',
      positions:new Float32Array([-1,-1,0,1,-1,0,0,1,0]),
      normals:new Float32Array([0,0,1,0,0,1,0,0,1]),
      uvs:new Float32Array([0,0,1,0,.5,1]),
      indices:new Uint32Array([0,1,2]),
      materialIndices:new Uint16Array([0])
    },0);
    store.finalize();
    const acceleration=new RenderAccelerationSet(store,new RenderBVH(store,1));
    if(ShadowVisibility.visible(acceleration,[0,0,-1],[0,0,1],10)!==false){
      errors.push('Shadow occlusion test failed');
    }
    if(ShadowVisibility.visible(acceleration,[3,3,-1],[0,0,1],10)!==true){
      errors.push('Shadow clear test failed');
    }
    const occRay=new RenderRay([0,0,-1],[0,0,1],1e-4,10);
    if(acceleration.occluded(occRay)!==true)errors.push('BVH any-hit test failed');
  }

  {
    const light=new RenderSceneLight({
      id:'r',name:'r',type:'rectangle',enabled:true,color:'#ffffff',intensity:10,
      range:0,size:1,width:2,height:2,coneAngle:90,penumbra:0,castShadows:true,
      position:[0,0,5],direction:[0,0,-1]
    });
    const s=AreaLightSampler.sample(light,[0,0,0],.5,.5);
    if(!s||Math.abs(s.distance-5)>1e-4||s.radiance.some(x=>!Number.isFinite(x)||x<0)){
      errors.push('Rectangle area sample failed');
    }
  }

  {
    const a=RenderSampling.sample2D(123,4),b=RenderSampling.sample2D(123,4);
    if(a[0]!==b[0]||a[1]!==b[1])errors.push('Deterministic sampling test failed');
  }

  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}
window.runRenderGroup7SelfTests=runRenderGroup7SelfTests;

function validateRenderGroup7(){
  const errors=[];
  if(typeof ShadowVisibility.visible!=='function')errors.push('ShadowVisibility missing');
  if(typeof AreaLightSampler.sample!=='function')errors.push('AreaLightSampler missing');
  if(typeof DirectLightingIntegrator.evaluate!=='function')errors.push('DirectLightingIntegrator missing');
  if(typeof EnvironmentSampler.background!=='function')errors.push('EnvironmentSampler missing');
  const tests=runRenderGroup7SelfTests();if(!tests.ok)errors.push(...tests.errors);
  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}

  AreaLightSampler,DirectLightingIntegrator,RenderGroup7Contract

var RenderCoreD=Object.freeze({
  version:'3.90.0-core-d',

  evaluateSurfaceDirect(job,acceleration,compiled,ray,hit,pixelSeed,options={}){
    return DirectLightingIntegrator.evaluate(
      job,acceleration,compiled,ray,hit,pixelSeed,options
    );
  },

  evaluateEnvironment(job,compiled,rayDirection){
    return EnvironmentSampler.background(
      job.renderScene,job.settings,compiled.lights,rayDirection
    );
  },

  tracePath(job,acceleration,compiled,ray,pixelSeed,initialHit=null){
    return PathIntegrator.trace(
      job,acceleration,compiled,ray,pixelSeed,initialHit
    );
  },

  visible(acceleration,origin,direction,maxDistance){
    return ShadowVisibility.visible(acceleration,origin,direction,maxDistance);
  },

  validate(){
    const errors=[];
    const g7=validateRenderGroup7();if(!g7.ok)errors.push(...g7.errors);
    const g8=validateRenderGroup8();if(!g8.ok)errors.push(...g8.errors);
    if(typeof this.evaluateSurfaceDirect!=='function')errors.push('Core D direct evaluator missing');
    if(typeof this.tracePath!=='function')errors.push('Core D path tracer missing');

function runRenderGroup7SelfTests(){
  const errors=[];

  {
    const store=new RenderTriangleStore();
    store.addPacket({
      objectId:'shadow-test',
      positions:new Float32Array([-1,-1,0,1,-1,0,0,1,0]),
      normals:new Float32Array([0,0,1,0,0,1,0,0,1]),
      uvs:new Float32Array([0,0,1,0,.5,1]),
      indices:new Uint32Array([0,1,2]),
      materialIndices:new Uint16Array([0])
    },0);
    store.finalize();
    const acceleration=new RenderAccelerationSet(store,new RenderBVH(store,1));
    if(ShadowVisibility.visible(acceleration,[0,0,-1],[0,0,1],10)!==false){
      errors.push('Shadow occlusion test failed');
    }
    if(ShadowVisibility.visible(acceleration,[3,3,-1],[0,0,1],10)!==true){
      errors.push('Shadow clear test failed');
    }
    const occRay=new RenderRay([0,0,-1],[0,0,1],1e-4,10);
    if(acceleration.occluded(occRay)!==true)errors.push('BVH any-hit test failed');
  }

  {
    const light=new RenderSceneLight({
      id:'r',name:'r',type:'rectangle',enabled:true,color:'#ffffff',intensity:10,
      range:0,size:1,width:2,height:2,coneAngle:90,penumbra:0,castShadows:true,
      position:[0,0,5],direction:[0,0,-1]
    });
    const s=AreaLightSampler.sample(light,[0,0,0],.5,.5);
    if(!s||Math.abs(s.distance-5)>1e-4||s.radiance.some(x=>!Number.isFinite(x)||x<0)){
      errors.push('Rectangle area sample failed');
    }
  }

  {
    const a=RenderSampling.sample2D(123,4),b=RenderSampling.sample2D(123,4);
    if(a[0]!==b[0]||a[1]!==b[1])errors.push('Deterministic sampling test failed');
  }

  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}
window.runRenderGroup7SelfTests=runRenderGroup7SelfTests;

const GGXImportanceSampler=Object.freeze({
  sampleHalfVector(n,roughness,u1,u2){
    const a=Math.max(.002025,roughness*roughness);
    const a2=a*a;
    const phi=2*Math.PI*u1;
    const cosTheta=Math.sqrt(Math.max(0,(1-u2)/(1+(a2-1)*u2)));
    const sinTheta=Math.sqrt(Math.max(0,1-cosTheta*cosTheta));
    const basis=LightBasis.fromDirection(n);
    return RayMath.normalize(
      RayMath.add(
        RayMath.add(RayMath.mul(basis.tangent,sinTheta*Math.cos(phi)),RayMath.mul(basis.bitangent,sinTheta*Math.sin(phi))),
        RayMath.mul(basis.n,cosTheta)
      )
    );
  },
  sampleVisibleHalfVector(n,v,roughness,u1,u2){
    const a=Math.max(.002025,roughness*roughness);
    const basis=LightBasis.fromDirection(n);
    const vl=[RayMath.dot(v,basis.tangent),RayMath.dot(v,basis.bitangent),Math.max(1e-6,RayMath.dot(v,basis.n))];
    const vh=RayMath.normalize([a*vl[0],a*vl[1],vl[2]]);
    const lensq=vh[0]*vh[0]+vh[1]*vh[1];
    const t1=lensq>1e-12?[-vh[1]/Math.sqrt(lensq),vh[0]/Math.sqrt(lensq),0]:[1,0,0];
    const t2=RayMath.cross(vh,t1);
    const r=Math.sqrt(Math.max(0,u1)),phi=2*Math.PI*u2;
    const p1=r*Math.cos(phi);
    let p2=r*Math.sin(phi);
    const mix=.5*(1+vh[2]);
    p2=(1-mix)*Math.sqrt(Math.max(0,1-p1*p1))+mix*p2;
    const z=Math.sqrt(Math.max(0,1-p1*p1-p2*p2));
    const nh=RayMath.add(RayMath.add(RayMath.mul(t1,p1),RayMath.mul(t2,p2)),RayMath.mul(vh,z));
    const ne=RayMath.normalize([a*nh[0],a*nh[1],Math.max(0,nh[2])]);
    return RayMath.normalize(RayMath.add(RayMath.add(RayMath.mul(basis.tangent,ne[0]),RayMath.mul(basis.bitangent,ne[1])),RayMath.mul(basis.n,ne[2])));
  },
  reflect(i,n){return RayMath.sub(i,RayMath.mul(n,2*RayMath.dot(i,n)));},
  distribution(nDotH,roughness){
    const a=Math.max(.002025,roughness*roughness),a2=a*a;
    const d=nDotH*nDotH*(a2-1)+1;
    return a2/(Math.PI*d*d+1e-8);
  }
});

      return RenderColor.add(material.baseColor,RenderColor.mul(material.emissive,material.emissiveIntensity));
    }

    const n=SurfaceInterpolator.normal(acceleration,hit);
    const v=RayMath.mul(ray.direction,-1);
    let color=RenderColor.mul(material.emissive,material.emissiveIntensity);

    for(const light of lights){
      const sample=DirectLightSampler.sample(light,hit.position);
      if(sample.kind==='ambient'){
        const diffuse=RenderColor.mul(material.baseColor,(1-material.metalness)/Math.PI);
        color=RenderColor.add(color,RenderColor.mul(RenderColor.mul(diffuse,sample.radiance),Math.PI));
      }else{
        const brdf=PhysicalBSDF.evaluate(material,n,v,sample.direction);
        color=RenderColor.add(color,RenderColor.mul(brdf,sample.radiance));
      }
    }
    return color;
  },

  async render(job,acceleration,onProgress=()=>{}){
    const rr=RenderRegion14.resolve(job,640,360);
    const width=rr.width,height=rr.height;

    const rgba=new Uint8ClampedArray(width*height*4);
    const aovs=new RenderAOVSet(width,height);
    const compiled=RenderPart4Core.compile(job);
    const backgroundFallback=RenderColor.hexToLinear(job.settings.background||'#202020');
    const perf16=RenderCoreH.attachPerformance(job);
    const primaryBatch16=new RenderRayBatch16(Math.max(32,RenderCoreH.tileSize(job)*RenderCoreH.tileSize(job)));
    const rowChunk=RenderCoreH.yieldRows(job);
    const renderStart16=RenderProfiler16.begin();
    LiteTraceExecutionContext.activeJob=job;LiteTraceExecutionContext.performance=perf16;

    const tiles16=RenderTileScheduler16.makeTiles(width,height,RenderCoreH.tileSize(job));
    await RenderTileScheduler16.runLocal(job,tiles16,async tile=>{
      for(let y=tile.y;y<tile.y+tile.height;y++){
        for(let x=tile.x;x<tile.x+tile.width;x++){
          const ray=RenderCameraRayGenerator.ray(
            job.renderScene.camera,
            rr.x0+x,rr.y0+y,
            rr.fullW,rr.fullH
          );
          const hit=acceleration.trace(ray);
          const o=(y*width+x)*4;
  
          if(hit.hit){
            const pixelSeed=((y*width+x+1)>>>0);
            const linear=RenderPart4Core.evaluate(job,acceleration,compiled,ray,hit,pixelSeed);
            RenderAOVUtil.writePrimary(aovs,y*width+x,RenderAOVUtil.primary(job,acceleration,compiled,ray,hit,pixelSeed));
            aovs.set3('Beauty',y*width+x,linear);
            aovs.set3('Global Illumination',y*width+x,[0,0,0]);
            aovs.set1('Noise',y*width+x,0);
            const mapped=RenderColor.linearToSrgb(RenderColor.aces(linear));
            rgba[o]=Math.round(RenderColor.clamp01(mapped[0])*255);
            rgba[o+1]=Math.round(RenderColor.clamp01(mapped[1])*255);
            rgba[o+2]=Math.round(RenderColor.clamp01(mapped[2])*255);
            rgba[o+3]=255;
          }else{
            const env=RenderPart4Core.environment(job,compiled,ray.direction)||backgroundFallback;
            RenderAOVUtil.writePrimary(aovs,y*width+x,null);
            aovs.set3('Beauty',y*width+x,env);
            aovs.set3('Global Illumination',y*width+x,env);
            aovs.set1('Noise',y*width+x,0);
            const mapped=RenderColor.linearToSrgb(RenderColor.aces(env));
            rgba[o]=Math.round(RenderColor.clamp01(mapped[0])*255);
            rgba[o+1]=Math.round(RenderColor.clamp01(mapped[1])*255);
            rgba[o+2]=Math.round(RenderColor.clamp01(mapped[2])*255);
            rgba[o+3]=job.settings.transparent?0:255;
          }
        }
      }
      try{window.ThreeDLiteRenderWindow3979?.tile?.(job,width,height,rgba,tile,{total:tiles16.length});}catch(_){}
      perf16.yields++;
    },onProgress);
    RenderCoreH.markStage(job,'render',renderStart16);
    if(LiteTraceExecutionContext.performance===perf16){LiteTraceExecutionContext.activeJob=null;LiteTraceExecutionContext.performance=null;}
    job.renderChannels=aovs;
    return {
      width,height,rgba,
      channels:aovs,
      metadata:Object.freeze({
        renderer:'Part 4 Fast Direct',
        materialCount:compiled.materials.list.length,
        lightCount:compiled.lights.length,
        toneMapping:'ACES',
        colorSpace:'sRGB output / linear shading',
        diagnostics:RenderPart4Core.diagnostics(job,compiled,acceleration),
        performance:RenderCoreH.performance(job)
      })
    };
  }
});

var RenderCoreC=Object.freeze({
  version:'3.79.09-core-c',

  compileScene(renderScene,settings){
    const materials=RenderMaterialLibrary.compile(renderScene);
    const lights=BasicRenderLighting.create(renderScene,settings);
    return Object.freeze({materials,lights});
  },

  evaluateDirect(job,acceleration,compiled,ray,hit,pixelSeed=1,options={}){
    return DirectLightingIntegrator.evaluate(job,acceleration,compiled,ray,hit,pixelSeed,options);
  },

  validate(){
    const errors=[];
    const g5=validateRenderGroup5();if(!g5.ok)errors.push(...g5.errors);
    const g6=validateRenderGroup6();if(!g6.ok)errors.push(...g6.errors);
    if(typeof this.compileScene!=='function')errors.push('Core C scene compiler missing');
    if(typeof this.evaluateDirect!=='function')errors.push('Core C direct evaluator missing');
    return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
  }
});
window.RenderCoreC=RenderCoreC;

class RenderSceneLight{
  constructor(snapshot){
    this.id=String(snapshot.id||'light');
    this.name=String(snapshot.name||'Light');
    this.type=RenderLightPolicy.type(snapshot.type);
    this.enabled=snapshot.enabled!==false;
    this.color=Object.freeze(RenderColor.hexToLinear(snapshot.color||'#ffffff'));
    this.intensity=RenderLightPolicy.scalar(snapshot.intensity,0,0,1e12);
    this.range=RenderLightPolicy.scalar(snapshot.range,0,0,1e12);
    this.size=RenderLightPolicy.scalar(snapshot.size,1,RenderLightPolicy.minLinearSize,1e9);
    this.width=RenderLightPolicy.scalar(snapshot.width,1,RenderLightPolicy.minLinearSize,1e9);
    this.height=RenderLightPolicy.scalar(snapshot.height,1,RenderLightPolicy.minLinearSize,1e9);
    this.coneAngle=RenderLightPolicy.scalar(snapshot.coneAngle,45,1,179)*Math.PI/180;
    this.penumbra=RenderLightPolicy.scalar(snapshot.penumbra,0,0,1);
    this.castShadows=snapshot.castShadows!==false;
    this.position=Object.freeze(RenderLightPolicy.finite3(snapshot.position,[0,0,0]));
    const rawDir=RenderLightPolicy.finite3(snapshot.direction,[0,0,-1]);
    let dir=RayMath.normalize(rawDir);
    if(!dir.every(Number.isFinite)||RayMath.length(dir)<1e-10)dir=[0,0,-1];
    this.direction=Object.freeze(dir);
    Object.freeze(this);
  }
}

var SceneLightCompiler=Object.freeze({
  compile(renderScene){
    const compiled=[];
    for(const snapshot of (renderScene?.lights||[])){
      if(snapshot?.enabled===false)continue;
      try{compiled.push(new RenderSceneLight(snapshot));}
      catch(err){throw new LightError(`${snapshot?.name||snapshot?.id||'Light'}: ${String(err?.message||err)}`,err);}
    }
    return Object.freeze(compiled);
  }
});

const DirectLightSampler=Object.freeze({
  sample(light,point){
    if(light.type==='dome'){
      return {kind:'ambient',direction:null,distance:Infinity,radiance:LightEnergyPolicy.radiance(light)};
    }
    if(light.type==='sun'){
      return {kind:'directional',direction:RayMath.mul(light.direction,-1),distance:Infinity,radiance:LightEnergyPolicy.radiance(light)};
    }

    const to=RayMath.sub(light.position,point);
    const dist=Math.max(RayMath.length(to),1e-4);
    const dir=RayMath.mul(to,1/dist);
    let attenuation=LightEnergyPolicy.distanceAttenuation(light,dist);
    if(attenuation<=0)return {kind:'local',direction:dir,distance:dist,radiance:[0,0,0]};

    if(light.type==='spot'){
      const fromLight=RayMath.mul(dir,-1);
      const cosTheta=RayMath.dot(light.direction,fromLight);
      const outer=Math.cos(light.coneAngle*.5);
      const inner=Math.cos(light.coneAngle*.5*(1-light.penumbra));
      const cone=inner<=outer?(cosTheta>=outer?1:0):Math.max(0,Math.min(1,(cosTheta-outer)/(inner-outer)));
      if(cone<=0)return {kind:'local',direction:dir,distance:dist,radiance:[0,0,0]};
      attenuation*=cone;
    }

    return {
      kind:'local',
      direction:dir,
      distance:dist,
      radiance:LightEnergyPolicy.radiance(light,attenuation)
    };
  }
});

function updateSelectedLightPanel(){
  const section=document.getElementById('lightModifySection');
  if(!section)return;
  const obj=selected;
  const isLight=EditorLightSystem.isLightObject(obj);
  section.style.display=isLight?'':'none';
  if(!isLight)return;

  const l=obj.userData.renderLight;
  const set=(id,v)=>{const e=document.getElementById(id);if(e)e.value=v};
  set('lightTypeReadout',l.type);
  set('lightIntensity',l.intensity);
  set('lightColor',l.color);
  set('lightRange',l.range);
  set('lightSize',l.size);
  set('lightWidth',l.width);
  set('lightHeight',l.height);
  set('lightConeAngle',l.coneAngle);
  set('lightPenumbra',l.penumbra);
  const en=document.getElementById('lightEnabled');if(en)en.checked=l.enabled!==false;
  const sh=document.getElementById('lightCastShadows');if(sh)sh.checked=l.castShadows!==false;
}

function markRenderLightDirty(){
  window.__3DLiteLightRevision=(window.__3DLiteLightRevision||0)+1;
  requestRender();
}

function bindLightUI(){
  document.querySelectorAll('[data-light-create]').forEach(btn=>{
    btn.addEventListener('click',()=>{
      EditorLightSystem.create(btn.dataset.lightCreate,{
        intensity:Number(document.getElementById('newLightIntensity')?.value)||10,
        color:document.getElementById('newLightColor')?.value||'#ffffff',
        size:Number(document.getElementById('newLightSize')?.value)||25,
        range:Number(document.getElementById('newLightRange')?.value)||0
      });
    });
  });

  const bindings=[
    ['lightIntensity','intensity','number'],
    ['lightColor','color','string'],
    ['lightRange','range','number'],
    ['lightSize','size','number'],
    ['lightWidth','width','number'],
    ['lightHeight','height','number'],
    ['lightConeAngle','coneAngle','number'],
    ['lightPenumbra','penumbra','number']
  ];

  for(const [id,key,kind] of bindings){
    document.getElementById(id)?.addEventListener('change',e=>{
      if(!EditorLightSystem.isLightObject(selected))return;
      selected.userData.renderLight[key]=kind==='number'?Number(e.target.value):e.target.value;
      /* v3.98.3 viewport icon is independent of physical size/color */
      markRenderLightDirty();
    });
  }

  document.getElementById('lightEnabled')?.addEventListener('change',e=>{
    if(EditorLightSystem.isLightObject(selected)){
      selected.userData.renderLight.enabled=!!e.target.checked;
      markRenderLightDirty();
    }
  });
  document.getElementById('lightCastShadows')?.addEventListener('change',e=>{
    if(EditorLightSystem.isLightObject(selected)){
      selected.userData.renderLight.castShadows=!!e.target.checked;
      markRenderLightDirty();
    }
  });
}

function runRenderGroup6SelfTests(){
  const errors=[];
  const point=new RenderSceneLight({
    id:'p',name:'p',type:'point',enabled:true,color:'#ffffff',intensity:10,
    range:0,size:1,width:1,height:1,coneAngle:45,penumbra:0,
    castShadows:true,position:[0,0,10],direction:[0,0,-1]
  });
  const ps=DirectLightSampler.sample(point,[0,0,0]);
  if(ps.kind!=='local'||Math.abs(ps.distance-10)>1e-5||ps.radiance.some(x=>!Number.isFinite(x)||x<0))errors.push('Point light sample failed');

  const sun=new RenderSceneLight({
    id:'s',name:'s',type:'sun',enabled:true,color:'#ffffff',intensity:1,
    range:0,size:1,width:1,height:1,coneAngle:.53,penumbra:0,
    castShadows:true,position:[0,0,0],direction:[0,0,-1]
  });
  const ss=DirectLightSampler.sample(sun,[0,0,0]);
  if(ss.kind!=='directional'||Math.abs(ss.direction[2]-1)>1e-5)errors.push('Sun direction sample failed');

  const spot=new RenderSceneLight({
    id:'sp',name:'sp',type:'spot',enabled:true,color:'#ffffff',intensity:10,
    range:100,size:1,width:1,height:1,coneAngle:45,penumbra:.2,
    castShadows:true,position:[0,0,10],direction:[0,0,-1]
  });
  const sp=DirectLightSampler.sample(spot,[0,0,0]);
  if(sp.radiance.some(x=>!Number.isFinite(x)||x<0)||sp.radiance.every(x=>x<=0))errors.push('Spot light on-axis sample failed');
  const spOutside=DirectLightSampler.sample(spot,[10,0,0]);
  if(spOutside.radiance.some(x=>!Number.isFinite(x))||spOutside.radiance.some(x=>x!==0))errors.push('Spot light outside-cone cutoff failed');

  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}

const RenderSampling=Object.freeze({
  hash32(x){
    x=(x|0)+0x6D2B79F5|0;
    x=Math.imul(x^(x>>>15),x|1);
    x^=x+Math.imul(x^(x>>>7),x|61);
    return ((x^(x>>>14))>>>0)/4294967296;
  },
  sample2D(seed,index){
    return [
      this.hash32((seed*73856093)^(index*19349663)),
      this.hash32((seed*83492791)^(index*2971215073))
    ];
  },
  concentricDisk(u1,u2){
    const sx=2*u1-1,sy=2*u2-1;
    if(sx===0&&sy===0)return [0,0];
    let r,theta;
    if(Math.abs(sx)>Math.abs(sy)){
      r=sx;theta=(Math.PI/4)*(sy/sx);
    }else{
      r=sy;theta=(Math.PI/2)-(Math.PI/4)*(sx/sy);
    }
    return [r*Math.cos(theta),r*Math.sin(theta)];
  }
});

const LightBasis=Object.freeze({
  fromDirection(direction){
    const n=RayMath.normalize(direction);
    const helper=Math.abs(n[2])<.999?[0,0,1]:[0,1,0];
    const tangent=RayMath.normalize(RayMath.cross(helper,n));
    const bitangent=RayMath.cross(n,tangent);
    return {n,tangent,bitangent};
  },
  pointOnRectangle(light,u,v){
    const b=this.fromDirection(light.direction);
    const du=(u-.5)*light.width,dv=(v-.5)*light.height;
    return RayMath.add(light.position,RayMath.add(RayMath.mul(b.tangent,du),RayMath.mul(b.bitangent,dv)));
  },
  pointOnDisc(light,u,v){
    const b=this.fromDirection(light.direction);
    const d=RenderSampling.concentricDisk(u,v);
    const r=light.size*.5;
    return RayMath.add(light.position,RayMath.add(RayMath.mul(b.tangent,d[0]*r),RayMath.mul(b.bitangent,d[1]*r)));
  },
  pointOnSphere(light,u,v){
    const z=1-2*u;
    const r=Math.sqrt(Math.max(0,1-z*z));
    const phi=2*Math.PI*v;
    const local=[r*Math.cos(phi),r*Math.sin(phi),z];
    return RayMath.add(light.position,RayMath.mul(local,light.size*.5));
  }
});

const EnvironmentSampler=Object.freeze({
  findDome(lights){return (lights||[]).find(l=>l.enabled!==false&&l.type==='dome')||null;},
  radiance(light,direction){
    if(!light||light.enabled===false||light.type!=='dome')return [0,0,0];
    return LightEnergyPolicy.radiance(light);
  },
  background(renderScene,settings,lights,rayDirection){
    const dome=this.findDome(lights);
    if(dome)return this.radiance(dome,rayDirection);
    return RenderColor.hexToLinear(settings.background||'#202020');
  }
});

const ShadowVisibility=Object.freeze({
  visible(acceleration,origin,direction,maxDistance){
    try{LiteTraceExecutionContext.performance?.addRay?.(RenderRayType.SHADOW);}catch(_){}
    const eps=RenderRayPolicy.epsilonForPoint(origin);
    if(Number.isFinite(maxDistance)&&maxDistance<=eps*2)return true;
    const o=RayMath.add(origin,RayMath.mul(direction,eps));
    const limit=Number.isFinite(maxDistance)?Math.max(eps,maxDistance-eps):Infinity;
    const ray=new RenderRay(o,direction,eps,limit,RenderRayType.SHADOW,0);
    return !acceleration.occluded(ray);
  }
});

      return this._local(light,point,lp,RenderLightPolicy.area(light

/* ============================================================
   3DLite v3.82.8 — LiteTrace Renderer v1.1 RC1 ASI Fix — GROUP 7
   Shadows + Area Sampling + Environment
   ============================================================ */

const RenderSampling=Object.freeze({
  hash32(x){
    x=(x|0)+0x6D2B79F5|0;
    x=Math.imul(x^(x>>>15),x|1);
    x^=x+Math.imul(x^(x>>>7),x|61);
    return ((x^(x>>>14))>>>0)/4294967296;
  },
  sample2D(seed,index){
    return [
      this.hash32((seed*73856093)^(index*19349663)),
      this.hash32((seed*83492791)^(index*2971215073))
    ];
  },
  concentricDisk(u1,u2){
    const sx=2*u1-1,sy=2*u2-1;
    if(sx===0&&sy===0)return [0,0];
    let r,theta;
    if(Math.abs(sx)>Math.abs(sy)){
      r=sx;theta=(Math.PI/4)*(sy/sx);
    }else{
      r=sy;theta=(Math.PI/2)-(Math.PI/4)*(sx/sy);
    }
    return [r*Math.cos(theta),r*Math.sin(theta)];
  }
});

const LightBasis=Object.freeze({
  fromDirection(direction){
    const n=RayMath.normalize(direction);
    const helper=Math.abs(n[2])<.999?[0,0,1]:[0,1,0];
    const tangent=RayMath.normalize(RayMath.cross(helper,n));
    const bitangent=RayMath.cross(n,tangent);
    return {n,tangent,bitangent};
  },
  pointOnRectangle(light,u,v){
    const b=this.fromDirection(light.direction);
    const du=(u-.5)*light.width,dv=(v-.5)*light.height;
    return RayMath.add(light.position,RayMath.add(RayMath.mul(b.tangent,du),RayMath.mul(b.bitangent,dv)));
  },
  pointOnDisc(light,u,v){
    const b=this.fromDirection(light.direction);
    const d=RenderSampling.concentricDisk(u,v);
    const r=light.size*.5;
    return RayMath.add(light.position,RayMath.add(RayMath.mul(b.tangent,d[0]*r),RayMath.mul(b.bitangent,d[1]*r)));
  },
  pointOnSphere(light,u,v){
    const z=1-2*u;
    const r=Math.sqrt(Math.max(0,1-z*z));
    const phi=2*Math.PI*v;
    const local=[r*Math.cos(phi),r*Math.sin(phi),z];
    return RayMath.add(light.position,RayMath.mul(local,light.size*.5));
  }
});

const EnvironmentSampler=Object.freeze({
  findDome(lights){return (lights||[]).find(l=>l.enabled!==false&&l.type==='dome')||null;},
  radiance(light,direction){
    if(!light||light.enabled===false||light.type!=='dome')return [0,0,0];
    return LightEnergyPolicy.radiance(light);
  },
  background(renderScene,settings,lights,rayDirection){
    const dome=this.findDome(lights);
    if(dome)return this.radiance(dome,rayDirection);
    return RenderColor.hexToLinear(settings.background||'#202020');
  }
});

const ShadowVisibility=Object.freeze({
  visible(acceleration,origin,direction,maxDistance){
    try{LiteTraceExecutionContext.performance?.addRay?.(RenderRayType.SHADOW);}catch(_){}
    const eps=RenderRayPolicy.epsilonForPoint(origin);
    if(Number.isFinite(maxDistance)&&maxDistance<=eps*2)return true;
    const o=RayMath.add(origin,RayMath.mul(direction,eps));
    const limit=Number.isFinite(maxDistance)?Math.max(eps,maxDistance-eps):Infinity;
    const ray=new RenderRay(o,direction,eps,limit,RenderRayType.SHADOW,0);
    return !acceleration.occluded(ray);
  }
});

const AreaLightSampler=Object.freeze({
  sample(light,point,u=.5,v=.5){
    if(light.type==='rectangle'){
      const lp=LightBasis.pointOnRectangle(light,u,v);
      return this._local(light,point,lp,RenderLightPolicy.area(light),light.direction);
    }
    if(light.type==='disc'){
      const lp=LightBasis.pointOnDisc(light,u,v);
      return this._local(light,point,lp,RenderLightPolicy.area(light),light.direction);
    }
    if(light.type==='sphere'){
      const lp=LightBasis.pointOnSphere(light,u,v);
      const normal=RayMath.normalize(RayMath.sub(lp,light.position));
      return this._local(light,point,lp,RenderLightPolicy.area(light),normal);
-- hit 5 line 7213 --
      markRenderLightDirty();
    }
  });
}

const RenderGroup6Contract=Object.freeze({
  owns:Object.freeze([
    'editor render-light objects',
    'Point/Spot/Rectangle/Disc/Sphere/Sun/Dome/Mesh light data',
    'light creation UI',
    'light modify UI',
    'immutable light snapshot extraction',
    'renderer light compilation',
    'direct light sampling interface'
  ]),
  consumes:Object.freeze([
    'editor transform system',
    'Core A RenderScene snapshots',
    'Group 5 direct BRDF'
  ]),
  doesNotOwn:Object.freeze([
    'shadow-ray visibility',
    'area-light stochastic sampling',
    'path GI',
    'light cache',
    'path guiding'
  ])
});

function runRenderGroup6SelfTests(){
  const errors=[];
  const point=new RenderSceneLight({
    id:'p',name:'p',type:'point',enabled:true,color:'#ffffff',intensity:10,
    range:0,size:1,width:1,height:1,coneAngle:45,penumbra:0,
    castShadows:true,position:[0,0,10],direction:[0,0,-1]
  });
  const ps=DirectLightSampler.sample(point,[0,0,0]);
  if(ps.kind!=='local'||Math.abs(ps.distance-10)>1e-5||ps.radiance.some(x=>!Number.isFinite(x)||x<0))errors.push('Point light sample failed');

  const sun=new RenderSceneLight({
    id:'s',name:'s',type:'sun',enabled:true,color:'#ffffff',intensity:1,
    range:0,size:1,width:1,height:1,coneAngle:.53,penumbra:0,
    castShadows:true,position:[0,0,0],direction:[0,0,-1]
  });
  const ss=DirectLightSampler.sample(sun,[0,0,0]);
  if(ss.kind!=='directional'||Math.abs(ss.direction[2]-1)>1e-5)errors.push('Sun direction sample failed');

  const spot=new RenderSceneLight({
    id:'sp',name:'sp',type:'spot',enabled:true,color:'#ffffff',intensity:10,
    range:100,size:1,width:1,height:1,coneAngle:45,penumbra:.2,
    castShadows:true,position:[0,0,10],direction:[0,0,-1]
  });
  const sp=DirectLightSampler.sample(spot,[0,0,0]);
  if(sp.radiance.some(x=>!Number.isFinite(x)||x<0)||sp.radiance.every(x=>x<=0))errors.push('Spot light on-axis sample failed');
  const spOutside=DirectLightSampler.sample(spot,[10,0,0]);
  if(spOutside.radiance.some(x=>!Number.isFinite(x))||spOutside.radiance.some(x=>x!==0))errors.push('Spot light outside-cone cutoff failed');

  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}
window.runRenderGroup6SelfTests=runRenderGroup6SelfTests;

function validateRenderGroup6(){
  const errors=[];
  if(typeof EditorLightSystem.create!=='function')errors.push('EditorLightSystem missing');
  if(typeof SceneLightCompiler.compile!=='function')errors.push('SceneLightCompiler missing');
  if(typeof DirectLightSampler.sample!=='function')errors.push('DirectLightSampler missing');
  const tests=runRenderGroup6SelfTests();if(!tests.ok)errors.push(...tests.errors);
  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}

  _local(light,point,lightPoint,area,emitterNormal=null){
    const to=RayMath.sub(lightPoint,point);
    const dist=Math.max(RayMath.length(to),1e-6);
    const dir=RayMath.mul(to,1/dist);

    if(light.range>0&&dist>=light.range){
      return {kind:'local',direction:dir,distance:dist,radiance:[0,0,0],lightPoint,area};
    }

    let attenuation=LightEnergyPolicy.distanceAttenuation(light,dist);

    let emitterCos=1;
    if(emitterNormal){
      emitterCos=Math.max(0,RayMath.dot(RayMath.normalize(emitterNormal),RayMath.mul(dir,-1)));
      if(emitterCos<=0){
        return {kind:'local',direction:dir,distance:dist,radiance:[0,0,0],lightPoint,area,emitterCos:0};
      }
    }

    const safeArea=Math.max(RenderLightPolicy.minArea,area);
    return {
      kind:'local',
      direction:dir,
      distance:dist,
      // Size changes sampling footprint/softness only; intensity remains the same user-facing unit.
      radiance:LightEnergyPolicy.radiance(light,attenuation*emitterCos),
      lightPoint,
      area:safeArea,
      emitterCos
    };
  }
});

var DirectLightingIntegrator=Object.freeze({
  samplesFor(light,settings){
    if(['rectangle','disc','sphere','mesh'].includes(light.type)){
      const preset=String(settings?.quality||'Preview');
      if(preset==='Ultra')return 16;
      if(preset==='High')return 8;
      if(preset==='Draft')return 1;
      return 4;
    }
    return 1;
  },

  evaluate(job,acceleration,compiled,ray,hit,pixelSeed=1,options={}){
    const material=RenderMaterialLibrary.resolveHit(job.renderScene,compiled.materials,hit);
    const n=SurfaceInterpolator.normal(acceleration,hit);
    const v=RayMath.mul(ray.direction,-1);
    const includeEmission=options.includeEmission!==false;
    const includeDome=options.includeDome!==false;
    let color=includeEmission?RenderColor.mul(material.emissive,material.emissiveIntensity):[0,0,0];

    if(material.shading==='unlit'){
      return includeEmission?RenderColor.add(material.baseColor,color):[0,0,0];
    }

    const emissiveSet=compiled.emissiveTriangles||job.emissiveTriangles||null;
    if(emissiveSet?.triangles?.length){
      const es=EmissiveTriangleSet.sample(emissiveSet,pixelSeed,0,hit.position);
      if(es&&ShadowVisibility.visible(acceleration,hit.position,es.direction,es.distance)){
        const brdf=PhysicalBSDF.evaluate(material,n,v,es.direction);
        const bsdfPdf=PathBSDFSampler.pdf(material,n,v,es.direction);
        const w=RenderMIS.power(es.pdf,bsdfPdf);
        color=RenderColor.add(color,RenderColor.mul(RenderColor.mul(brdf,es.radiance),w));
      }
    }

    for(let li=0;li<compiled.lights.length;li++){
      const light=compiled.lights[li];

      if(light.type==='dome'){
        if(includeDome){
          const ambient=EnvironmentSampler.radiance(light,n);
          const diffuse=RenderColor.mul(material.baseColor,(1-material.metalness)/Math.PI);
          color=RenderColor.add(color,RenderColor.mul(RenderColor.mul(diffuse,ambient),Math.PI));
        }
        continue;
      }

      const count=this.samplesFor(light,job.settings);
      let accum=[0,0,0];

      for(let si=0;si<count;si++){
        const uv=RenderSampling.sample2D(pixelSeed^(li*2654435761),si);
        const sample=['rectangle','disc','sphere','mesh'].includes(light.type)
          ? AreaLightSampler.sample(light,hit.position,uv[0],uv[1])
          : DirectLightSampler.sample(light,hit.position);

        if(!sample||!sample.direction)continue;
        if(!sample.radiance||sample.radiance.every(v=>v<=0||!Number.isFinite(v)))continue;

        if(light.castShadows!==false){
          const visible=ShadowVisibility.visible(acceleration,hit.position,sample.direction,sample.distance);
          if(!visible)continue;
        }

        const brdf=PhysicalBSDF.evaluate(material,n,v,sample.direction);
        accum=RenderColor.add(accum,RenderColor.mul(brdf,sample.radiance));
      }

      if(count>1)accum=RenderColor.mul(accum,1/count);
      color=RenderColor.add(color,accum);
    }

    return color;
  }
});

      return this._local(light,point,lp,RenderLightPolicy.area(light),normal);

      // True emissive mesh-triangle sampling remains Group 10.

  _local(light,point,lightPoint,area,emitterNormal=null){
    const to=RayMath.sub(lightPoint,point);
    const dist=Math.max(RayMath.length(to),1e-6);
    const dir=RayMath.mul(to,1/dist);

    if(light.range>0&&dist>=light.range){
      return {kind:'local',direction:dir,distance:dist,radiance:[0,0,0],lightPoint,area};
    }

    let attenuation=LightEnergyPolicy.distanceAttenuation(light,dist);

    let emitterCos=1;
    if(emitterNormal){
      emitterCos=Math.max(0,RayMath.dot(RayMath.normalize(emitterNormal),RayMath.mul(dir,-1)));
      if(emitterCos<=0){
        return {kind:'local',direction:dir,distance:dist,radiance:[0,0,0],lightPoint,area,emitterCos:0};
      }
    }

    const safeArea=Math.max(RenderLightPolicy.minArea,area);
    return {
      kind:'local',
      direction:dir,
      distance:dist,
      // Size changes sampling footprint/softness only; intensity remains the same user-facing unit.
      radiance:LightEnergyPolicy.radiance(light,attenuation*emitterCos),
      lightPoint,
      area:safeArea,
      emitterCos
    };
  }
});

var DirectLightingIntegrator=Object.freeze({
  samplesFor(light,settings){
    if(['rectangle','disc','sphere','mesh'].includes(light.type)){
      const preset=String(settings?.quality||'Preview');
      if(preset==='Ultra')return 16;
      if(preset==='High')return 8;
      if(preset==='Draft')return 1;
      return 4;
    }
    return 1;
  },

  evaluate(job,acceleration,compiled,ray,hit,pixelSeed=1,options={}){
    const material=RenderMaterialLibrary.resolveHit(job.renderScene,compiled.materials,hit);
    const n=SurfaceInterpolator.normal(acceleration,hit);
    const v=RayMath.mul(ray.direction,-1);
    const includeEmission=options.includeEmission!==false;
    const includeDome=options.includeDome!==false;
    let color=includeEmission?RenderColor.mul(material.emissive,material.emissiveIntensity):[0,0,0];

    if(material.shading==='unlit'){
      return includeEmission?RenderColor.add(material.baseColor,color):[0,0,0];
    }

    const emissiveSet=compiled.emissiveTriangles||job.emissiveTriangles||null;
    if(emissiveSet?.triangles?.length){
      const es=EmissiveTriangleSet.sample(emissiveSet,pixelSeed,0,hit.position);
      if(es&&ShadowVisibility.visible(acceleration,hit.position,es.direction,es.distance)){
        const brdf=PhysicalBSDF.evaluate(material,n,v,es.direction);
        const bsdfPdf=PathBSDFSampler.pdf(material,n,v,es.direction);
        const w=RenderMIS.power(es.pdf,bsdfPdf);
        color=RenderColor.add(color,RenderColor.mul(RenderColor.mul(brdf,es.radiance),w));
      }
    }

    for(let li=0;li<compiled.lights.length;li++){
      const light=compiled.lights[li];

      if(light.type==='dome'){
        if(includeDome){
          const ambient=EnvironmentSampler.radiance(light,n);
          const diffuse=RenderColor.mul(material.baseColor,(1-material.metalness)/Math.PI);
          color=RenderColor.add(color,RenderColor.mul(RenderColor.mul(diffuse,ambient),Math.PI));
        }
        continue;
      }

      const count=this.samplesFor(light,job.settings);
      let accum=[0,0,0];

      for(let si=0;si<count;si++){
        const uv=RenderSampling.sample2D(pixelSeed^(li*2654435761),si);
        const sample=['rectangle','disc','sphere','mesh'].includes(light.type)
          ? AreaLightSampler.sample(light,hit.position,uv[0],uv[1])
          : DirectLightSampler.sample(light,hit.position);

        if(!sample||!sample.direction)continue;
        if(!sample.radiance||sample.radiance.every(v=>v<=0||!Number.isFinite(v)))continue;

        if(light.castShadows!==false){
          const visible=ShadowVisibility.visible(acceleration,hit.position,sample.direction,sample.distance);
          if(!visible)continue;
        }

        const brdf=PhysicalBSDF.evaluate(material,n,v,sample.direction);
        accum=RenderColor.add(accum,RenderColor.mul(brdf,sample.radiance));
      }

      if(count>1)accum=RenderColor.mul(accum,1/count);
      color=RenderColor.add(color,accum);
    }

    return color;
  }
});

const RenderAOVUtil=Object.freeze({
  idColor(id){
    const h=(RenderSampling.hash32((Number(id)||0)+1)*4294967296)>>>0;
    return [((h>>>16)&255)/255,((h>>>8)&255)/255,(h&255)/255];
  },
  primary(job,acceleration,compiled,ray,hit,pixelSeed){
    if(!hit?.hit)return null;
    const material=RenderMaterialLibrary.resolveHit(job.renderScene,compiled.materials,hit);
    const n=SurfaceInterpolator.normal(acceleration,hit);
    const emission=RenderColor.mul(material.emissive,material.emissiveIntensity);
    const direct=Group9DirectMIS.evaluate(job,acceleration,compiled,ray,hit,pixelSeed);
    const objId=hit.objectIndex??0,matId=hit.materialIndex??0;
    return {
      alpha:1,
      albedo:material.baseColor,
      diffuse:RenderColor.mul(material.baseColor,Math.max(0,1-material.metalness)),
      emission,
      normal:n,
      depth:Math.max(0,hit.t||0),
      objectId:this.idColor(objId),
      materialId:this.idColor((objId+1)*4099+matId),
      direct,
      specular:RenderColor.mul(direct,Math.max(.04,material.metalness+(1-material.roughness)*.25)),
      reflection:RenderColor.mul(direct,Math.max(0,material.metalness)),
      refraction:RenderColor.mul(direct,Math.max(0,material.transmission||0)),
      shadow:[0,0,0]
    };
  },
  writePrimary(set,i,a){
    if(!a){set.set1('Alpha',i,0);return;}
    set.set1('Alpha',i,a.alpha);
    set.set3('Albedo',i,a.albedo);set.set3('Diffuse',i,a.diffuse);set.set3('Emission',i,a.emission);
    set.set3('Normal',i,a.normal);set.set1('Depth',i,a.depth);
    set.set3('Object ID',i,a.objectId);set.set3('Material ID',i,a.materialId);
    set.set3('Direct Lighting',i,a.direct);set.set3('Specular',i,a.specular);
    set.set3('Reflection',i,a.reflection);set.set3('Refraction',i,a.refraction);set.set3('Shadow',i,a.shadow);
  }
});

const RenderPerformancePolicy16=Object.freeze({
  tileSize(job){
    const raw=String(job?.settings?.tileSize||'Auto');
    if(raw==='Auto'){
      const q=String(job?.settings?.quality||'Preview');
      const w=Math.max(1,job?.progress?.resolution?.[0]||640);
      const h=Math.max(1,job?.progress?.resolution?.[1]||360);
      const pixels=w*h;
      const threads=Math.max(1,Number(job?.progress?.threads)||Math.max(1,(navigator.hardwareConcurrency||2)-1));
      let size=q==='Draft'?32:q==='Ultra'?8:16;
      if(pixels>1920*1080&&threads>=8)size=Math.max(size,32);
      if(pixels<640*480)size=Math.min(size,16);
      if(threads<=2)size=Math.max(size,32);
      return size;
    }
    const n=Number(raw);
    return [8,16,32,64].includes(n)?n:16;
  },
  yieldRows(job){
    // Tile Size controls cooperative row batches only.
    // This deliberately does not replace the proven scanline traversal.
    return Math.max(1,Math.min(16,Math.round(this.tileSize(job)/4)));
  }
});

var RenderProfiler16=Object.freeze({
  now(){return performance.now();},
  begin(){return performance.now();},
  elapsed(start){return Math.max(0,performance.now()-start);},
  mark(counter,name,start){
    const ms=this.elapsed(start);
    if(counter?.stageMs&&name in counter.stageMs)counter.stageMs[name]+=ms;
    return ms;
  }
});
window.RenderProfiler16=RenderProfiler16;

var RenderControlToken16 = class RenderControlToken16{
  constructor(){
    this.shared=typeof SharedArrayBuffer!=='undefined'&&typeof Atomics!=='undefined';
    this.buffer=this.shared?new SharedArrayBuffer(Int32Array.BYTES_PER_ELEMENT*3):new ArrayBuffer(Int32Array.BYTES_PER_ELEMENT*3);
    this.flags=new Int32Array(this.buffer); // [cancel,pause,generation]
    this.flags[2]=1;
  }
  cancel(){this.shared?Atomics.store(this.flags,0,1):(this.flags[0]=1);}
  reset(){this.shared?(Atomics.store(this.flags,0,0),Atomics.store(this.flags,1,0),Atomics.add(this.flags,2,1)):(this.flags[0]=0,this.flags[1]=0,this.flags[2]++);}
  pause(){this.shared?Atomics.store(this.flags,1,1):(this.flags[1]=1);}
  resume(){this.shared?Atomics.store(this.flags,1,0):(this.flags[1]=0);}
  cancelled(){return (this.shared?Atomics.load(this.flags,0):this.flags[0])!==0;}
  paused(){return (this.shared?Atomics.load(this.flags,1):this.flags[1])!==0;}
  generation(){return this.shared?Atomics.load(this.flags,2):this.flags[2];}
}
window.RenderControlToken16=RenderControlToken16;

const LightTypes=Object.freeze(['point','spot','rectangle','disc','sphere','sun','dome','mesh']);

const LightDefaults=Object.freeze({

// v3.98.3: one artistic intensity scale for every light type.
// Intensity=1 is the common baseline. Local lights use a gentle scene-unit falloff
// so they remain useful instead of becoming nearly black from raw 1/r^2 at cm-scale distances.
const LightEnergyPolicy=Object.freeze({
  referenceDistance:100,
  distanceAttenuation(light,distance){
    const d=Math.max(0,Number(distance)||0);
    const ref=this.referenceDistance;
    let a=1/(1+(d/ref)*(d/ref));
    const range=Math.max(0,Number(light?.range)||0);
    if(range>0){
      if(d>=range)return 0;
      const t=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,1-d/range));
      // smooth finite-range fade without changing the shared intensity unit.
      a*=t*t*(3-2*t);
    }
    return a;
  },
  radiance(light,factor=1){
    const intensity=Math.max(0,Number(light?.intensity)||0);
    return RenderColor.mul(light.color,intensity*Math.max(0,Number(factor)||0));
  }
});
window.LightEnergyPolicy=LightEnergyPolicy;
window.__3DLiteLightEnergy3983=true;
window.__3DLiteLightDefaultDirection3983=true;
window.ThreeDLiteLightEnergy3983=Obje
-- hit 4 line 7389 --
(light),normal);
    }
    if(light.type==='mesh'){
      // Part 4 keeps Mesh Light as a rectangular proxy.
      // True emissive mesh-triangle sampling remains Group 10.
      const lp=LightBasis.pointOnRectangle(light,u,v);
      return this._local(light,point,lp,RenderLightPolicy.area(light),light.direction);
    }
    return DirectLightSampler.sample(light,point);
  },

  _local(light,point,lightPoint,area,emitterNormal=null){
    const to=RayMath.sub(lightPoint,point);
    const dist=Math.max(RayMath.length(to),1e-6);
    const dir=RayMath.mul(to,1/dist);

    if(light.range>0&&dist>=light.range){
      return {kind:'local',direction:dir,distance:dist,radiance:[0,0,0],lightPoint,area};
    }

    let attenuation=LightEnergyPolicy.distanceAttenuation(light,dist);

    let emitterCos=1;
    if(emitterNormal){
      emitterCos=Math.max(0,RayMath.dot(RayMath.normalize(emitterNormal),RayMath.mul(dir,-1)));
      if(emitterCos<=0){
        return {kind:'local',direction:dir,distance:dist,radiance:[0,0,0],lightPoint,area,emitterCos:0};
      }
    }

    const safeArea=Math.max(RenderLightPolicy.minArea,area);
    return {
      kind:'local',
      direction:dir,
      distance:dist,
      // Size changes sampling footprint/softness only; intensity remains the same user-facing unit.
      radiance:LightEnergyPolicy.radiance(light,attenuation*emitterCos),
      lightPoint,
      area:safeArea,
      emitterCos
    };
  }
});

var DirectLightingIntegrator=Object.freeze({
  samplesFor(light,settings){
    if(['rectangle','disc','sphere','mesh'].includes(light.type)){
      const preset=String(settings?.quality||'Preview');
      if(preset==='Ultra')return 16;
      if(preset==='High')return 8;
      if(preset==='Draft')return 1;
      return 4;
    }
    return 1;
  },

  evaluate(job,acceleration,compiled,ray,hit,pixelSeed=1,options={}){
    const material=RenderMaterialLibrary.resolveHit(job.renderScene,compiled.materials,hit);
    const n=SurfaceInterpolator.normal(acceleration,hit);
    const v=RayMath.mul(ray.direction,-1);
    const includeEmission=options.includeEmission!==false;
    const includeDome=options.includeDome!==false;
    let color=includeEmission?RenderColor.mul(material.emissive,material.emissiveIntensity):[0,0,0];

    if(material.shading==='unlit'){
      return includeEmission?RenderColor.add(material.baseColor,color):[0,0,0];
    }

    const emissiveSet=compiled.emissiveTriangles||job.emissiveTriangles||null;
    if(emissiveSet?.triangles?.length){
      const es=EmissiveTriangleSet.sample(emissiveSet,pixelSeed,0,hit.position);
      if(es&&ShadowVisibility.visible(acceleration,hit.position,es.direction,es.distance)){
        const brdf=PhysicalBSDF.evaluate(material,n,v,es.direction);
        const bsdfPdf=PathBSDFSampler.pdf(material,n,v,es.direction);
        const w=RenderMIS.power(es.pdf,bsdfPdf);
        color=RenderColor.add(color,RenderColor.mul(RenderColor.mul(brdf,es.radiance),w));
      }
    }

    for(let li=0;li<compiled.lights.length;li++){
      const light=compiled.lights[li];

      if(light.type==='dome'){
        if(includeDome){
          const ambient=EnvironmentSampler.radiance(light,n);
          const diffuse=RenderColor.mul(material.baseColor,(1-material.metalness)/Math.PI);
          color=RenderColor.add(color,RenderColor.mul(RenderColor.mul(diffuse,ambient),Math.PI));
        }
        continue;
      }

      const count=this.samplesFor(light,job.settings);
      let accum=[0,0,0];

      for(let si=0;si<count;si++){
        const uv=RenderSampling.sample2D(pixelSeed^(li*2654435761),si);
        const sample=['rectangle','disc','sphere','mesh'].includes(light.type)
          ? AreaLightSampler.sample(light,hit.position,uv[0],uv[1])
          : DirectLightSampler.sample(light,hit.position);

        if(!sample||!sample.direction)continue;
        if(!sample.radiance||sample.radiance.every(v=>v<=0||!Number.isFinite(v)))continue;

        if(light.castShadows!==false){
          const visible=ShadowVisibility.visible(acceleration,hit.position,sample.direction,sample.distance);
          if(!visible)continue;
        }

        const brdf=PhysicalBSDF.evaluate(material,n,v,sample.direction);
        accum=RenderColor.add(accum,RenderColor.mul(brdf,sample.radiance));
      }

      if(count>1)accum=RenderColor.mul(accum,1/count);
      color=RenderColor.add(color,accum);
    }

    return color;
  }
});

const GuidedBSDFSampler=Object.freeze({
  sample(job,material,n,v,position,seed,bounce){
    const field=job?.pathGuide;
    if(!PathGuidePolicy.enabled(job)||!field)return PathBSDFSampler.sample(material,n,v,seed,bounce);
    const guide=field.lookup(position,n);
    if(!guide)return PathBSDFSampler.sample(material,n,v,seed,bounce);

    const mix=Math.min(PathGuidePolicy.maxMix,.15+.3*guide.confidence);
    const choose=PathSampler.scalar(seed,bounce,42);
    let direction,lobe='guided';
    if(choose<mix){
      const gs=field.sample(position,n,seed,bounce);
      if(!gs)return PathBSDFSampler.sample(material,n,v,seed,bounce);
      direction=gs.direction;
    }else{
      const bs=PathBSDFSampler.sample(material,n,v,seed,bounce);
      if(!bs||bs.transmission)return bs;
      direction=bs.direction;lobe=bs.lobe;
    }
    const bsdfPdf=PathBSDFSampler.pdf(material,n,v,direction);
    const guidePdf=field.pdf(position,n,direction);
    const pdf=Math.max(1e-8,(1-mix)*bsdfPdf+mix*guidePdf);
    const value=PhysicalBSDF.evaluate(material,n,v,direction);
    return {direction,pdf,weight:RenderColor.mul(value,1/pdf),lobe,guided:true};
  }
});


const Group9DirectMIS=Object.freeze({
  lightPdf(light,sample){
    if(!light||!sample)return 0;
    if(['point','spot','sun'].includes(light.type))return Infinity; // delta lights: light sampling owns the event
    if(['rectangle','disc','sphere','mesh'].includes(light.type)){
      const area=Math.max(RenderLightPolicy.minArea,Number(sample.area)||RenderLightPolicy.area(light));
      const distance=Math.max(1e-6,Number(sample.distance)||1);
      const cosEmitter=Math.max(1e-6,Number(sample.emitterCos)||1);
      return (distance*distance)/(area*cosEmitter);
    }
    return 0;
  },

  evaluate(job,acceleration,compiled,ray,hit,pixelSeed=1){
    const material=RenderMaterialLibrary.resolveHit(job.renderScene,compiled.materials,hit);
    const n=SurfaceInterpolator.normal(acceleration,hit);
    const v=RayMath.mul(ray.direction,-1);
    let color=RenderColor.mul(material.emissive,material.emissiveIntensity);
    const maxShadowRays=Math.max(1,Math.min(4096,Number(job?.settings?.maxShadowRaysPerHit)||1024));
    let shadowRays=0;

    if(material.shading==='unlit')return RenderColor.add(material.baseColor,color);

    const useTree=LightTreePolicy.enabled(job,compiled)&&job.lightTree;
    const selected=useTree?job.lightTree.sample(hit.position,pixelSeed^0x51ED270B):null;
    const candidates=selected
      ? [{light:selected.light,sourceIndex:selected.sourceIndex,selectionPdf:selected.pdf}]
      : compiled.lights.map((light,sourceIndex)=>({light,sourceIndex,selectionPdf:1}));

    for(const candidate of candidates){
      const light=candidate.light;
      if(!light||light.type==='dome')continue;

      const li=candidate.sourceIndex;
      const selectionPdf=Math.max(1e-8,candidate.selectionPdf||1);
      const count=DirectLightingIntegrator.samplesFor(light,job.settings);
      let accum=[0,0,0];

      for(let si=0;si<count;si++){
        const uv=RenderSampling.sample2D(pixelSeed^(li*2654435761),si);
        const sample=['rectangle','disc','sphere','mesh'].includes(light.type)
          ? AreaLightSampler.sample(light,hit.position,uv[0],uv[1])
          : DirectLightSampler.sample(light,hit.position);

        if(!sample?.direction||!sample.radiance||sample.radiance.every(x=>x<=0||!Number.isFinite(x)))continue;
        if(light.castShadows!==false){
          if(shadowRays>=maxShadowRays)break;
          shadowRays++;
          if(!ShadowVisibility.visible(acceleration,hit.position,sample.direction,sample.distance))continue;
        }

        const scaledRadiance=useTree?RenderColor.mul(sample.radiance,1/selectionPdf):sample.radiance;
        const brdf=PhysicalBSDF.evaluate(material,n,v,sample.direction);
        const bsdfPdf=PathBSDFSampler.pdf(material,n,v,sample.direction);
        const conditionalPdf=this.lightPdf(light,sample);
        const lightPdf=Number.isFinite(conditionalPdf)?conditionalPdf*selectionPdf:conditionalPdf;
        const weight=Number.isFinite(lightPdf)?RenderMIS.power(lightPdf,bsdfPdf):1;
        accum=RenderColor.add(accum,RenderColor.mul(RenderColor.mul(brdf,scaledRadiance),weight));
      }

      if(count>1)accum=RenderColor.mul(accum,1/count);
      color=RenderColor.add(color,accum);
    }
    return color;
  }
});

const LightCachePolicy=Object.freeze({
  sceneScale(acceleration){
    const root=acceleration?.bvh?.root;
    const node=(root!=null&&root>=0)?acceleration.bvh.nodes[root]:null;
    if(!node)return 1;
    const d=RayMath.sub(node.max,node.min);
    return Math.max(1e-4,RayMath.length(d));
  },
  radius(job,acceleration){
    const q=String(job?.settings?.quality||'Preview');
    const divisor=q==='Ultra'?64:q==='High'?48:q==='Draft'?20:32;
    return Math.max(RenderRayPolicy.epsilonForPoint([0,0,0])*32,this.sceneScale(acceleration)/divisor);
  },
  probes(job){
    const q=String(job?.settings?.quality||'Preview');
    return q==='Ultra'?16:q==='High'?12:q==='Draft'?3:8;
  },
  maxRecords(job){
    const q=String(job?.settings?.quality||'Preview');
    return q==='Ultra'?12000:q==='High'?8000:q==='Draft'?1500:4000;
  },
  retraceEvery(job){
    const q=String(job?.settings?.quality||'Preview');
    return q==='Ultra'?8:q==='High'?12:q==='Draft'?32:16;
  },
  normalThreshold:.75
});

  try{
    const invalid=new RenderSceneLight({
      id:'bad',name:'bad',type:'unsupported',enabled:true,color:'#ffffff',
      intensity:NaN,range:-1,size:0,width:0,height:0,coneAngle:999,penumbra:-2,
      castShadows:true,position:[NaN,0,0],direction:[0,0,0]
    });
    if(invalid.type!=='point'||invalid.intensity!==0||invalid.range!==0||
       invalid.size<RenderLightPolicy.minLinearSize||!invalid.direction.every(Number.isFinite)){
      errors.push('Light sanitization test failed');
    }
  }catch(err){errors.push('Light sanitization test: '+String(err?.message||err));}

  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}

function validateRenderPart4(){
  const errors=[];
  if(typeof RenderLightPolicy?.area!=='function')errors.push('RenderLightPolicy missing');
  if(typeof SceneLightCompiler?.compile!=='function')errors.push('SceneLightCompiler missing');
  if(typeof ShadowVisibility?.visible!=='function')errors.push('ShadowVisibility missing');
  if(typeof AreaLightSampler?.sample!=='function')errors.push('AreaLightSampler missing');
  if(typeof EnvironmentSampler?.background!=='function')errors.push('EnvironmentSampler missing');
  if(typeof BasicDirectRenderer?.render!=='function')errors.push('Fast renderer missing');
  if(typeof FastRenderDiagnostics?.summarize!=='function')errors.push('Fast render diagnostics missing');
  const t=runRenderPart4SelfTests();if(!t.ok)errors.push(...t.errors);
  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}

var RenderPart4Core=Object.freeze({
  version:'3.79.18-part4-gen3',
  compile(job){return job.shadingContext||RenderCoreC.compileScene(job.renderScene,job.settings);},
  evaluate(job,acceleration,compiled,ray,hit,pixelSeed=1){
    return RenderCoreD.evaluateSurfaceDirect(job,acceleration,compiled,ray,hit,pixelSeed,{includeDome:true,includeEmission:true});
  },
  environment(job,compiled,dir){return RenderCoreD.evaluateEnvironment(job,compiled,dir);},
  diagnostics(job,compiled,acceleration){return FastRenderDiagnostics.summarize(job,compiled,acceleration);},
  validate:validateRenderPart4
});
Object.assign(window,{RenderPart4Contract,FastRenderDiagnostics,runRenderPart4SelfTests,validateRenderPart4,RenderPart4Core});

    if(!finite3(out)||out.some(v=>v<0))errors.push('GGX grazing-angle finite test failed');
  }catch(err){errors.push('GGX finite test: '+String(err?.message||err));}

      errors.push('Camera center-ray test failed');

### SurfaceInterpolator
-- hit 1 line 6175 --
f0[0])*m,
      f0[1]+(1-f0[1])*m,
      f0[2]+(1-f0[2])*m
    ];
  },
  distributionGGX(nDotH,roughness){
    const a=roughness*roughness,a2=a*a;
    const d=nDotH*nDotH*(a2-1)+1;
    return a2/Math.max(Math.PI*d*d,1e-7);
  },
  geometrySchlickGGX(nDotV,roughness){
    const r=roughness+1,k=(r*r)/8;
    return nDotV/Math.max(nDotV*(1-k)+k,1e-7);
  },
  geometrySmith(nDotV,nDotL,roughness){
    return this.geometrySchlickGGX(nDotV,roughness)*this.geometrySchlickGGX(nDotL,roughness);
  },
  evaluate(material,n,v,l){
    if(material.shading==='unlit')return material.baseColor;
    if(!material||![...n,...v,...l].every(Number.isFinite))return [0,0,0];

    const h=RayMath.normalize(RayMath.add(v,l));
    if(!h.every(Number.isFinite))return [0,0,0];
    const nDotL=Math.max(0,RayMath.dot(n,l));
    const nDotV=Math.max(0,RayMath.dot(n,v));
    const nDotH=Math.max(0,RayMath.dot(n,h));
    const vDotH=Math.max(0,RayMath.dot(v,h));
    if(nDotL<=0||nDotV<=0)return [0,0,0];

    const dielectricF0=[.04,.04,.04];
    const f0=RenderColor.mix(dielectricF0,material.baseColor,material.metalness);
    const F=this.fresnelSchlick(vDotH,f0);
    const D=this.distributionGGX(nDotH,material.roughness);
    const G=this.geometrySmith(nDotV,nDotL,material.roughness);

    const specScale=D*G/Math.max(4*nDotV*nDotL,1e-6);
    const spec=RenderColor.mul(F,specScale);

    const kD=[
      (1-F[0])*(1-material.metalness),
      (1-F[1])*(1-material.metalness),
      (1-F[2])*(1-material.metalness)
    ];
    const diffuse=[
      kD[0]*material.baseColor[0]/Math.PI,
      kD[1]*material.baseColor[1]/Math.PI,
      kD[2]*material.baseColor[2]/Math.PI
    ];
    const out=RenderColor.mul(RenderColor.add(diffuse,spec),nDotL);
    return out.map(x=>Number.isFinite(x)&&x>=0?x:0);
  }
});

const SurfaceInterpolator=Object.freeze({
  normal(acceleration,hit){
    const s=acceleration.store,t=hit.triangleIndex,k=t*3;
    if(!s.normals||s.normals.length!==s.positions.length)return hit.geometricNormal;
    const ia=s.triangles[k],ib=s.triangles[k+1],ic=s.triangles[k+2];
    const w=1-hit.u-hit.v;
    const read=i=>[s.normals[i*3],s.normals[i*3+1],s.normals[i*3+2]];
    const a=read(ia),b=read(ib),c=read(ic);
    let n=RayMath.normalize([
      a[0]*w+b[0]*hit.u+c[0]*hit.v,
      a[1]*w+b[1]*hit.u+c[1]*hit.v,
      a[2]*w+b[2]*hit.u+c[2]*hit.v
    ]);
    if(!n.every(Number.isFinite)||RayMath.length(n)<1e-10)n=hit.geometricNormal;
    if(RayMath.dot(n,hit.geometricNormal)<0)n=RayMath.mul(n,-1);
    return n;
  },
  uv(acceleration,hit){
    const s=acceleration.store,t=hit.triangleIndex,k=t*3;
    if(!s.uvs||s.uvs.length!==(s.positions.length/3)*2)return [0,0];
    const ia=s.triangles[k],ib=s.triangles[k+1],ic=s.triangles[k+2],w=1-hit.u-hit.v;
    return [
      s.uvs[ia*2]*w+s.uvs[ib*2]*hit.u+s.uvs[ic*2]*hit.v,
      s.uvs[ia*2+1]*w+s.uvs[ib*2+1]*hit.u+s.uvs[ic*2+1]*hit.v
    ];
  }
});

const RenderAOVNames=Object.freeze([
  'Beauty','Alpha','Diffuse','Direct Lighting','Global Illumination',
  'Reflection','Refraction','Specular','Emission','Shadow',
  'Normal','Depth','Albedo','Object ID','Material ID','Noise'
]);

class RenderAOVSet{
  constructor(width,height){
    this.width=width;this.height=height;
    this.channels=new Map();
    this.meta=new Map();
  }
  ensure(name,components=3){
    if(!this.channels.has(name)){
      this.channels.set(name,new Float32Array(this.width*this.height*components));
      this.meta.set(name,{components});
    }
    return this.channels.get(name);
  }
  set3(name,index,v){
    const a=this.ensure(name,3),o=index*3;
    a[o]=Number.isFinite(v?.[0])?v[0]:0;
    a[o+1]=Number.isFinite(v?.[1])?v[1]:0;
    a[o+2]=Number.isFinite(v?.[2])?v[2]:0;
  }
  set1(name,index,v){
    const a=this.ensure(name,1);a[index]=Number.isFinite(v)?v:0;
  }
  get(name){return this.channels.get(name)||null}
  has(name){return this.channels.has(name)}
  names(){return [...this.channels.keys()]}
  toFrame(name,{depthMax=100,noiseMax=.1}={}){
    const data=this.channels.get(name);if(!data)return null;
    const comp=this.meta.get(name)?.components||3;
    const rgba=new Uint8ClampedArray(this.width*this.height*4);
    for(let i=0;i<this.width*this.height;i++){
      let c=[0,0,0],a=255;
      if(comp===1){
        let v=data[i];
        if(name==='Alpha'){v=RenderColor.clamp01(v);c=[v,v,v];a=Math.round(v*255);}
        else if(name==='Depth'){v=1-Math.min(1,Math.max(0,v/Math.max(1e-6,depthMax)));c=[v,v,v];}
        else if(name==='Noise'){v=Math.min(1,Math.max(0,v/Math.max(1e-6,noiseMax)));c=[v,v,v];}
        else {v=RenderColor.clamp01(v);c=[v,v,v];}
      }else{
        const o=i*3;c=[data[o],data[o+1],data[o+2]];
        if(name==='Normal')c=[c[0]*.5+.5,c[1]*.5+.5,c[2]*.5+.5];
        else if(['Object ID','Material ID'].includes(name))c=c.map(RenderColor.clamp01);
        else c=RenderColor.linearToSrgb(RenderColor.aces(c));
      }
      rgba[i*4]=Math.round(RenderColor.clamp01(c[0])*255);
      rgba[i*4+1]=Math.round(RenderColor.clamp01(c[1])*255);
      rgba[i*4+2]=Math.round(RenderColor.clamp01(c[2])*255);
      rgba[i*4+3]=a;
    }
    return {width:this.width,height:this.height,rgba,metadata:Object.freeze({channel:name})};
  }
}

const RenderAOVUtil=Object.freeze({
  idColor(id){
    const h=(RenderSampling.hash32((Number(id)||0)+1)*4294967296)>>>0;
    return [((h>>>16)&255)/255,((h>>>8)&255)/255,(h&255)/255];
  },
  primary(job,acceleration,compiled,ray,hit,pixelSeed){
    if(!hit?.hit)return null;
    const material=RenderMaterialLibrary.resolveHit(job.renderScene,compiled.materials,hit);
    const n=SurfaceInterpolator.normal(acceleration,hit);
    const emission=RenderColor.mul(material.emissive,material.emissiveIntensity);
    const direct=Group9DirectMIS.evaluate(job,acceleration,compiled,ray,hit,pixelSeed);
    const objId=hit.objectIndex??0,matId=hit.materialIndex??0;
    return {
      alpha:1,
      albedo:material.baseColor,
      diffuse:RenderColor.mul(material.baseColor,Math.max(0,1-material.metalness)),
      emission,
      normal:n,
      depth:Math.max(0,hit.t||0),
      objectId:this.idColor(objId),
      materialId:this.idColor((objId+1)*4099+matId),
      direct,
      specular:RenderColor.mul(direct,Math.max(.04,material.metalness+(1-material.roughness)*.25)),
      reflection:RenderColor.mul(direct,Math.max(0,material.metalness)),
      refraction:RenderColor.mul(direct,Math.max(0,material.transmission||0)),
      shadow:[0,0,0]
    };
  },
  writePrimary(set,i,a){
    if(!a){set.set1('Alpha',i,0);return;}
    set.set1('Alpha',i,a.alpha);
    set.set3('Albedo',i,a.albedo);set.set3('Diffuse',i,a.diffuse);set.set3('Emission',i,a.emission);
    set.set3('Normal',i,a.normal);set.set1('Depth',i,a.depth);
    set.set3('Object ID',i,a.objectId);set.set3('Material ID',i,a.materialId);
    set.set3('Direct Lighting',i,a.direct);set.set3('Specular',i,a.specular);
    set.set3('Reflection',i,a.reflection);set.set3('Refraction',i,a.refraction);set.set3('Shadow',i,a.shadow);
  }
});

/* ============================================================
   3DLite / LiteTrace — GROUP 16 GEN3 SAFE REWRITE
   RENDERER PERFORMANCE ARCHITECTURE
   Preserves existing Fast/Path traversal and startup behavior.
   ============================================================ */

const RenderPerformancePolicy16=Object.freeze({
  tileSize(job){
    const raw=String(job?.settings?.tileSize||'Auto');
    if(raw==='Auto'){
      const q=String(job?.settings?.quality||'Preview');
      const w=Math.max(1,job?.progress?.resolution?.[0]||640);
      const h=Math.max(1,job?.progress?.resolution?.[1]||360);
      const pixels=w*h;
      const threads=Math.max(1,Number(job?.progress?.threads)||Math.max(1,(navigator.hardwareConcurrency||2)-1));
      let size=q==='Draft'?32:q==='Ultra'?8:16;
      if(pixels>1920*1080&&threads>=8)size=Math.max(size,32);
      if(pixels<640*480)size=Math.min(size,16);
      if(threads<=2)size=Math.max(size,32);
      return size;
    }
    const n=Number(raw);
    return [8,16,32,64].includes(n)?n:16;
  },
  yieldRows(job){
    // Tile Size controls cooperative row batches only.
    // This deliberately does not replace the proven scanline traversal.
    return Math.max(1,Math.min(16,Math.round(this.tileSize(job)/4)));
  }
});



const LiteTraceScratch=Object.freeze({
  v3a:new Float64Array(3),v3b:new Float64Array(3),v3c:new Float64Array(3),rgb:new Float64Array(3)
});
window.LiteTraceScratch=LiteTraceScratch;

var RenderProfiler16=Object.freeze({
  now(){return performance.now();},
  begin(){return performance.now();},
  elapsed(start){return Math.max(0,performance.now()-start);},
  mark(counter,name,start){
    const ms=this.elapsed(start);
    if(counter?.stageMs&&name in counter.stageMs)counter.stageMs[name]+=ms;
    return ms;
  }
});
window.RenderProfiler16=RenderProfiler16;

var RenderControlToken16 = class RenderControlToken16{
  constructor(){
    this.shared=typeof SharedArrayBuffer!=='undefined'&&typeof Atomics!=='undefined';
    this.buffer=this.shared?new SharedArrayBuffer(Int32Array.BYTES_PER_ELEMENT*3):new ArrayBuffer(Int32Array.BYTES_PER_ELEMENT*3);
    this.flags=new Int32Array(this.buffer); // [cancel,pause,generation]
    this.flags[2]=1;
  }
  cancel(){this.shared?Atomics.store(this.flags,0,1):(this.flags[0]=1);}
  reset(){this.shared?(Atomics.store(this.flags,0,0),Atomics.store(this.flags,1,0),Atomics.add(this.flags,2,1)):(this.flags[0]=0,this.flags[1]=0,this.flags[2]++);}
  pause(){this.shared?Atomics.store(this.flags,1,1):(this.flags[1]=1);}
  resume(){this.shared?Atomics.store(this.flags,1,0):(this.flags[1]=0);}
  cancelled(){return (this.shared?Atomics.load(this.flags,0):this.flags[0])!==0;}
  paused(){return (this.shared?Atomics.load(this.flags,1):this.flags[1])!==0;}
  generation(){return this.shared?Atomics.load(this.flags,2):this.flags[2];}
}
window.RenderControlToken16=RenderControlToken16;

var RenderPerformanceCounters16 = class RenderPerformanceCounters16{
  constructor(){
    this.cameraRays=0;
    this.pathRays=0;
    this.shadowRays=0;
    this.probeRays=0;
    this.samples=0;
    this.yields=0;
    this.stageMs={snapshot:0,geometry:0,worker:0,bvh:0,shading:0,lightCache:0,render:0,denoise:0,toneMap:0,post:0};
    this.startedAt=RenderProfiler16.now();
  }
  addRay(type){
    if(type===RenderRayType.CAMERA)this.cameraRays++;
    else if(type===RenderRayType.SHADOW)this.shadowRays++;
    else this.pathRays++;
  }
  addProbe(){this.probeRays++;}

window.RenderWorkQueue16=RenderWorkQueue16;

var RenderTileScheduler16=Object.freeze({
  makeTiles(width,height,tileSize){
    const size=Math.max(4,Math.min(128,Number(tileSize)||16));
    const tiles=[];
    let id=0;
    for(let y=0;y<height;y+=size){
      for(let x=0;x<width;x+=size){
        tiles.push(Object.freeze({
          id:id++,x,y,
          width:Math.min(size,width-x),
          height:Math.min(size,height-y)
        }));
      }
    }
    return tiles;
  },
  async runLocal(job,tiles,runTile,onProgress=()=>{}){
    const total=Math.max(1,tiles.length);
    for(let i=0;i<tiles.length;i++){
      if(job.cancelled)throw Object.assign(new Error('Render job cancelled'),{cancelled:true});
      while(job.paused&&!job.cancelled)await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,20));
      await runTile(tiles[i],i);
      onProgress((i+1)/total);
      if((i&1)===1)await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,0));
    }
  }
});
window.RenderTileScheduler16=RenderTileScheduler16;

var LiteTraceExecutionContext=(function(){

  const ctx={activeJob:null,performance:null};
  window.LiteTraceExecutionContext=ctx;
  return ctx;
})();
