var BasicDirectRenderer=Object.freeze({
  shade(job,acceleration,materials,lights,ray,hit){
    const material=RenderMaterialLibrary.resolveHit(job.renderScene,materials,hit);
    if(material.shading==='unlit'){
      return RenderColor.add(material.baseColor,RenderColor.mul(material.emissive,material.emissiveIntensity));
    }

    const n=SurfaceInterpolator.normal(acceleration,hit);
    const v=RayMath.mul(ray.direction,-1);
    let color=RenderColor.mul(material.emissive,material.emissiveIntensity);

    for(const light of lights){
      const sample=DirectLightSampler.sample(light,hit.position);
      if(sample.kind==='ambient'){
        const diffuse=RenderColor.mul(material.baseColor,(1-material.metalness)/Math.PI);
        color=RenderColor.add(color,RenderColor.mul(RenderColor.mul(diffuse,sample.radiance),Math.PI));
      }else{
        const brdf=PhysicalBSDF.evaluate(material,n,v,sample.direction);
        color=RenderColor.add(color,RenderColor.mul(brdf,sample.radiance));
      }
    }
    return color;
  },

  async render(job,acceleration,onProgress=()=>{}){
    const rr=RenderRegion14.resolve(job,640,360);
    const width=rr.width,height=rr.height;

    const rgba=new Uint8ClampedArray(width*height*4);
    const aovs=new RenderAOVSet(width,height);
    const compiled=RenderPart4Core.compile(job);
    const backgroundFallback=RenderColor.hexToLinear(job.settings.background||'#202020');
    const perf16=RenderCoreH.attachPerformance(job);
    const primaryBatch16=new RenderRayBatch16(Math.max(32,RenderCoreH.tileSize(job)*RenderCoreH.tileSize(job)));
    const rowChunk=RenderCoreH.yieldRows(job);
    const renderStart16=RenderProfiler16.begin();
    LiteTraceExecutionContext.activeJob=job;LiteTraceExecutionContext.performance=perf16;

    const tiles16=RenderTileScheduler16.makeTiles(width,height,RenderCoreH.tileSize(job));
    await RenderTileScheduler16.runLocal(job,tiles16,async tile=>{
      for(let y=tile.y;y<tile.y+tile.height;y++){
        for(let x=tile.x;x<tile.x+tile.width;x++){
          const ray=RenderCameraRayGenerator.ray(
            job.renderScene.camera,
            rr.x0+x,rr.y0+y,
            rr.fullW,rr.fullH
          );
          const hit=acceleration.trace(ray);
          const o=(y*width+x)*4;
  
          if(hit.hit){
            const pixelSeed=((y*width+x+1)>>>0);
            const linear=RenderPart4Core.evaluate(job,acceleration,compiled,ray,hit,pixelSeed);
            RenderAOVUtil.writePrimary(aovs,y*width+x,RenderAOVUtil.primary(job,acceleration,compiled,ray,hit,pixelSeed));
            aovs.set3('Beauty',y*width+x,linear);
            aovs.set3('Global Illumination',y*width+x,[0,0,0]);
            aovs.set1('Noise',y*width+x,0);
            const mapped=RenderColor.linearToSrgb(RenderColor.aces(linear));
            rgba[o]=Math.round(RenderColor.clamp01(mapped[0])*255);
            rgba[o+1]=Math.round(RenderColor.clamp01(mapped[1])*255);
            rgba[o+2]=Math.round(RenderColor.clamp01(mapped[2])*255);
            rgba[o+3]=255;
          }else{
            const env=RenderPart4Core.environment(job,compiled,ray.direction)||backgroundFallback;
            RenderAOVUtil.writePrimary(aovs,y*width+x,null);
            aovs.set3('Beauty',y*width+x,env);
            aovs.set3('Global Illumination',y*width+x,env);
            aovs.set1('Noise',y*width+x,0);
            const mapped=RenderColor.linearToSrgb(RenderColor.aces(env));
            rgba[o]=Math.round(RenderColor.clamp01(mapped[0])*255);
            rgba[o+1]=Math.round(RenderColor.clamp01(mapped[1])*255);
            rgba[o+2]=Math.round(RenderColor.clamp01(mapped[2])*255);
            rgba[o+3]=job.settings.transparent?0:255;
          }
        }
      }
      try{window.ThreeDLiteRenderWindow3979?.tile?.(job,width,height,rgba,tile,{total:tiles16.length});}catch(_){}
      perf16.yields++;
    },onProgress);
    RenderCoreH.markStage(job,'render',renderStart16);
    if(LiteTraceExecutionContext.performance===perf16){LiteTraceExecutionContext.activeJob=null;LiteTraceExecutionContext.performance=null;}
    job.renderChannels=aovs;
    return {
      width,height,rgba,
      channels:aovs,
      metadata:Object.freeze({
        renderer:'Part 4 Fast Direct',
        materialCount:compiled.materials.list.length,
        lightCount:compiled.lights.length,
        toneMapping:'ACES',
        colorSpace:'sRGB output / linear shading',
        diagnostics:RenderPart4Core.diagnostics(job,compiled,acceleration),
        performance:RenderCoreH.performance(job)
      })
    };
  }
});

function runRenderGroup5SelfTests(){
  const errors=[];
  const approx=(a,b,e=1e-4)=>Math.abs(a-b)<=e;

  const white=RenderColor.srgbToLinear([1,1,1]);
  if(!approx(white[0],1)||!approx(white[1],1)||!approx(white[2],1))errors.push('sRGB white conversion failed');

  const mat=new RenderSurfaceMaterial({
    id:'t',name:'t',shading:'physical',baseColor:[.8,.3,.2],
    roughness:.5,metalness:0,opacity:1,emissive:[0,0,0],emissiveIntensity:0
  });
  const brdf=PhysicalBSDF.evaluate(mat,[0,0,1],[0,0,1],[0,0,1]);
  if(brdf.some(x=>!Number.isFinite(x)||x<0))errors.push('Physical BRDF finite/positive test failed');

  const metallic=new RenderSurfaceMaterial({
    id:'m',name:'m',shading:'physical',baseColor:[.9,.7,.2],
    roughness:.25,metalness:1,opacity:1,emissive:[0,0,0],emissiveIntensity:0
  });
  const mb=PhysicalBSDF.evaluate(metallic,[0,0,1],[0,0,1],[0,0,1]);
  if(mb.some(x=>!Number.isFinite(x)||x<0))errors.push('Metallic BRDF test failed');

  const tm=RenderColor.aces([10,3,1]);
  if(tm.some(x=>x<0||x>1||!Number.isFinite(x)))errors.push('Tone mapping range test failed');

  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}
window.runRenderGroup5SelfTests=runRenderGroup5SelfTests;

function validateRenderGroup5(){
  const errors=[];
  if(typeof RenderSurfaceMaterial!=='function')errors.push('RenderSurfaceMaterial missing');
  if(typeof PhysicalBSDF.evaluate!=='function')errors.push('PhysicalBSDF missing');
  if(typeof BasicDirectRenderer.render!=='function')errors.push('BasicDirectRenderer missing');
  if(typeof SurfaceInterpolator.normal!=='function')errors.push('SurfaceInterpolator missing');
  const tests=runRenderGroup5SelfTests();if(!tests.ok)errors.push(...tests.errors);
  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}

// v3.98.3: one artistic intensity scale for every light type.
// Intensity=1 is the common baseline. Local lights use a gentle scene-unit falloff
// so they remain useful instead of becoming nearly black from raw 1/r^2 at cm-scale distances.
const LightEnergyPolicy=Object.freeze({
  referenceDistance:100,
  distanceAttenuation(light,distance){
    const d=Math.max(0,Number(distance)||0);
    const ref=this.referenceDistance;
    let a=1/(1+(d/ref)*(d/ref));
    const range=Math.max(0,Number(light?.range)||0);
    if(range>0){
      if(d>=range)return 0;
      const t=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,1-d/range));
      // smooth finite-range fade without changing the shared intensity unit.
      a*=t*t*(3-2*t);
    }
    return a;
  },
  radiance(light,factor=1){
    const intensity=Math.max(0,Number(light?.intensity)||0);
    return RenderColor.mul(light.color,intensity*Math.max(0,Number(factor)||0));
  }
});
window.LightEnergyPolicy=LightEnergyPolicy;
window.__3DLiteLightEnergy3983=true;
window.__3DLiteLightDefaultDirection3983=true;
window.ThreeDLiteLightEnergy3983=Object.freeze({version:'3.99.0',baselineIntensity:1,referenceDistance:100});

function validateRenderGroup5(){
  const errors=[];
  if(typeof RenderSurfaceMaterial!=='function')errors.push('RenderSurfaceMaterial missing');
  if(typeof PhysicalBSDF.evaluate!=='function')errors.push('PhysicalBSDF missing');
  if(typeof BasicDirectRenderer.render!=='function')errors.push('BasicDirectRenderer missing');
  if(typeof SurfaceInterpolator.normal!=='function')errors.push('SurfaceInterpolator missing');
  const tests=runRenderGroup5SelfTests();if(!tests.ok)errors.push(...tests.errors);
  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}

// v3.98.3: one artistic intensity scale for every light type.
// Intensity=1 is the common baseline. Local lights use a gentle scene-unit falloff
// so they remain useful instead of becoming nearly black from raw 1/r^2 at cm-scale distances.
const LightEnergyPolicy=Object.freeze({
  referenceDistance:100,
  distanceAttenuation(light,distance){
    const d=Math.max(0,Number(distance)||0);
    const ref=this.referenceDistance;
    let a=1/(1+(d/ref)*(d/ref));
    const range=Math.max(0,Number(light?.range)||0);
    if(range>0){
      if(d>=range)return 0;
      const t=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,1-d/range));
      // smooth finite-range fade without changing the shared intensity unit.
      a*=t*t*(3-2*t);
    }
    return a;
  },
  radiance(light,factor=1){
    const intensity=Math.max(0,Number(light?.intensity)||0);
    return RenderColor.mul(light.color,intensity*Math.max(0,Number(factor)||0));
  }
});
window.LightEnergyPolicy=LightEnergyPolicy;

const PhysicalBSDF=Object.freeze({
  fresnelSchlick(cosTheta,f0){
    const m=Math.pow(1-Math.max(0,Math.min(1,cosTheta)),5);
    return [
      f0[0]+(1-f0[0])*m,
      f0[1]+(1-f0[1])*m,
      f0[2]+(1-f0[2])*m
    ];
  },
  distributionGGX(nDotH,roughness){
    const a=roughness*roughness,a2=a*a;
    const d=nDotH*nDotH*(a2-1)+1;
    return a2/Math.max(Math.PI*d*d,1e-7);
  },
  geometrySchlickGGX(nDotV,roughness){
    const r=roughness+1,k=(r*r)/8;
    return nDotV/Math.max(nDotV*(1-k)+k,1e-7);
  },
  geometrySmith(nDotV,nDotL,roughness){
    return this.geometrySchlickGGX(nDotV,roughness)*this.geometrySchlickGGX(nDotL,roughness);
  },
  evaluate(material,n,v,l){
    if(material.shading==='unlit')return material.baseColor;
    if(!material||![...n,...v,...l].every(Number.isFinite))return [0,0,0];

    const h=RayMath.normalize(RayMath.add(v,l));
    if(!h.every(Number.isFinite))return [0,0,0];
    const nDotL=Math.max(0,RayMath.dot(n,l));
    const nDotV=Math.max(0,RayMath.dot(n,v));
    const nDotH=Math.max(0,RayMath.dot(n,h));
    const vDotH=Math.max(0,RayMath.dot(v,h));
    if(nDotL<=0||nDotV<=0)return [0,0,0];

    const dielectricF0=[.04,.04,.04];
    const f0=RenderColor.mix(dielectricF0,material.baseColor,material.metalness);
    const F=this.fresnelSchlick(vDotH,f0);
    const D=this.distributionGGX(nDotH,material.roughness);
    const G=this.geometrySmith(nDotV,nDotL,material.roughness);

    const specScale=D*G/Math.max(4*nDotV*nDotL,1e-6);
    const spec=RenderColor.mul(F,specScale);

    const kD=[
      (1-F[0])*(1-material.metalness),
      (1-F[1])*(1-material.metalness),
      (1-F[2])*(1-material.metalness)
    ];
    const diffuse=[
      kD[0]*material.baseColor[0]/Math.PI,
      kD[1]*material.baseColor[1]/Math.PI,
      kD[2]*material.baseColor[2]/Math.PI
    ];
    const out=RenderColor.mul(RenderColor.add(diffuse,spec),nDotL);
    return out.map(x=>Number.isFinite(x)&&x>=0?x:0);
  }
});

const SurfaceInterpolator=Object.freeze({
  normal(acceleration,hit){
    const s=acceleration.store,t=hit.triangleIndex,k=t*3;
    if(!s.normals||s.normals.length!==s.positions.length)return hit.geometricNormal;
    const ia=s.triangles[k],ib=s.triangles[k+1],ic=s.triangles[k+2];
    const w=1-hit.u-hit.v;
    const read=i=>[s.normals[i*3],s.normals[i*3+1],s.normals[i*3+2]];
    const a=read(ia),b=read(ib),c=read(ic);
    let n=RayMath.normalize([
      a[0]*w+b[0]*hit.u+c[0]*hit.v,
      a[1]*w+b[1]*hit.u+c[1]*hit.v,
      a[2]*w+b[2]*hit.u+c[2]*hit.v
    ]);
    if(!n.every(Number.isFinite)||RayMath.length(n)<1e-10)n=hit.geometricNormal;
    if(RayMath.dot(n,hit.geometricNormal)<0)n=RayMath.mul(n,-1);
    return n;
  },
  uv(acceleration,hit){
    const s=acceleration.store,t=hit.triangleIndex,k=t*3;
    if(!s.uvs||s.uvs.length!==(s.positions.length/3)*2)return [0,0];
    const ia=s.triangles[k],ib=s.triangles[k+1],ic=s.triangles[k+2],w=1-hit.u-hit.v;
    return [
      s.uvs[ia*2]*w+s.uvs[ib*2]*hit.u+s.uvs[ic*2]*hit.v,
      s.uvs[ia*2+1]*w+s.uvs[ib*2+1]*hit.u+s.uvs[ic*2+1]*hit.v
    ];
  }
});

class RenderAOVSet{
  constructor(width,height){

      this.channels.set(name,new Float32Array(this.width*this.height*components));

  get(name){return this.channels.get(name)||null}

var BasicDirectRenderer=Object.freeze({
  shade(job,acceleration,materials,lights,ray,hit){
    const material=RenderMaterialLibrary.resolveHit(job.renderScene,materials,hit);
    if(material.shading==='unlit'){
      return RenderColor.add(material.baseColor,RenderColor.mul(material.emissive,material.emissiveIntensity));
    }

    const n=SurfaceInterpolator.normal(acceleration,hit);
    const v=RayMath.mul(ray.direction,-1);
    let color=RenderColor.mul(material.emissive,material.emissiveIntensity);

    for(const light of lights){
      const sample=DirectLightSampler.sample(light,hit.position);
      if(sample.kind==='ambient'){
        const diffuse=RenderColor.mul(material.baseColor,(1-material.metalness)/Math.PI);
        color=RenderColor.add(color,RenderColor.mul(RenderColor.mul(diffuse,sample.radiance),Math.PI));
      }else{
        const brdf=PhysicalBSDF.evaluate(material,n,v,sample.direction);
        color=RenderColor.add(color,RenderColor.mul(brdf,sample.radiance));
      }
    }
    return color;
  },

var RenderCoreC=Object.freeze({
  version:'3.79.09-core-c',

  compileScene(renderScene,settings){
    const materials=RenderMaterialLibrary.compile(renderScene);
    const lights=BasicRenderLighting.create(renderScene,settings);
    return Object.freeze({materials,lights});
  },

  evaluateDirect(job,acceleration,compiled,ray,hit,pixelSeed=1,options={}){
    return DirectLightingIntegrator.evaluate(job,acceleration,compiled,ray,hit,pixelSeed,options);
  },

  validate(){
    const errors=[];
    const g5=validateRenderGroup5();if(!g5.ok)errors.push(...g5.errors);
    const g6=validateRenderGroup6();if(!g6.ok)errors.push(...g6.errors);
    if(typeof this.compileScene!=='function')errors.push('Core C scene compiler missing');
    if(typeof this.evaluateDirect!=='function')errors.push('Core C direct evaluator missing');
    return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
  }
});

function runRenderGroup5SelfTests(){
  const errors=[];
  const approx=(a,b,e=1e-4)=>Math.abs(a-b)<=e;

  const white=RenderColor.srgbToLinear([1,1,1]);
  if(!approx(white[0],1)||!approx(white[1],1)||!approx(white[2],1))errors.push('sRGB white conversion failed');

  const mat=new RenderSurfaceMaterial({
    id:'t',name:'t',shading:'physical',baseColor:[.8,.3,.2],
    roughness:.5,metalness:0,opacity:1,emissive:[0,0,0],emissiveIntensity:0
  });
  const brdf=PhysicalBSDF.evaluate(mat,[0,0,1],[0,0,1],[0,0,1]);
  if(brdf.some(x=>!Number.isFinite(x)||x<0))errors.push('Physical BRDF finite/positive test failed');

  const metallic=new RenderSurfaceMaterial({
    id:'m',name:'m',shading:'physical',baseColor:[.9,.7,.2],
    roughness:.25,metalness:1,opacity:1,emissive:[0,0,0],emissiveIntensity:0
  });
  const mb=PhysicalBSDF.evaluate(metallic,[0,0,1],[0,0,1],[0,0,1]);
  if(mb.some(x=>!Number.isFinite(x)||x<0))errors.push('Metallic BRDF test failed');

  const tm=RenderColor.aces([10,3,1]);
  if(tm.some(x=>x<0||x>1||!Number.isFinite(x)))errors.push('Tone mapping range test failed');

  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}
window.runRenderGroup5SelfTests=runRenderGroup5SelfTests;

function validateRenderGroup5(){
  const errors=[];
  if(typeof RenderSurfaceMaterial!=='function')errors.push('RenderSurfaceMaterial missing');
  if(typeof PhysicalBSDF.evaluate!=='function')errors.push('PhysicalBSDF missing');
  if(typeof BasicDirectRenderer.render!=='function')errors.push('BasicDirectRenderer missing');
  if(typeof SurfaceInterpolator.normal!=='function')errors.push('SurfaceInterpolator missing');
  const tests=runRenderGroup5SelfTests();if(!tests.ok)errors.push(...tests.errors);
  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}

Object.assign(window,{
  RenderColor,RenderSurfaceMaterial,RenderMaterialLibrary,
  RenderDirectionalLight,RenderAmbientLight,BasicRenderLighting,
  PhysicalBSDF,SurfaceInterpolator,BasicDirectRenderer,RenderGroup5Contract
});

  rectangle:Object.freeze({intensity:1,size:25,width:50,height:50,range:0,coneAngle:90,penumbra:0}),
  disc:Object.freeze({intensity:1,size:25,range:0,coneAngle:90,penumbra:0}),
  sphere:Object.freeze({intensity:1,size:25,range:0,coneAngle:180,penumbra:0}),
  sun:Object.freeze({intensity:1,size:.5,range:0,coneAngle:.53,penumbra:0}),
  dome:Object.freeze({intensity:1,size:100,range:0,coneAngle:180,penumbra:0}),
  mesh:Object.freeze({intensity:1,size:25,range:0,coneAngle:180,penumbra:0})
});

// v3.98.3: one artistic intensity scale for every light type.
// Intensity=1 is the common baseline. Local lights use a gentle scene-unit falloff
// so they remain useful instead of becoming nearly black from raw 1/r^2 at cm-scale distances.
const LightEnergyPolicy=Object.freeze({
  referenceDistance:100,
  distanceAttenuation(light,distance){
    const d=Math.max(0,Number(distance)||0);
    const ref=this.referenceDistance;
    let a=1/(1+(d/ref)*(d/ref));
    const range=Math.max(0,Number(light?.range)||0);
    if(range>0){
      if(d>=range)return 0;
      const t=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,1-d/range));
      // smooth finite-range fade without changing the shared intensity unit.
      a*=t*t*(3-2*t);
    }
    return a;
  },
  radiance(light,factor=1){
    const intensity=Math.max(0,Number(light?.intensity)||0);
    return RenderColor.mul(light.color,intensity*Math.max(0,Number(factor)||0));
  }
});
window.LightEnergyPolicy=LightEnergyPolicy;
window.__3DLiteLightEnergy3983=true;
window.__3DLiteLightDefaultDirection3983=true;
window.ThreeDLiteLightEnergy3983=Object.freeze({version:'3.99.0',baselineIntensity:1,referenceDistance:100});

  nextId:1,

        else if(plane==='xz'){p0=[Math.cos(a)*r,z,Math.sin(a)*r];p1=[Math.cos(b)*r,z,Math.sin(b)*r]}
        else {p0=[z,Math.cos(a)*r,Math.sin(a)*r];p1=[z,Math.cos(b)*r,Math.sin(b)*r]}
        add(p0,p1);
      }
    };

function runRenderGroup5SelfTests(){
  const errors=[];
  const approx=(a,b,e=1e-4)=>Math.abs(a-b)<=e;

  const white=RenderColor.srgbToLinear([1,1,1]);
  if(!approx(white[0],1)||!approx(white[1],1)||!approx(white[2],1))errors.push('sRGB white conversion failed');

  const mat=new RenderSurfaceMaterial({
    id:'t',name:'t',shading:'physical',baseColor:[.8,.3,.2],
    roughness:.5,metalness:0,opacity:1,emissive:[0,0,0],emissiveIntensity:0
  });
  const brdf=PhysicalBSDF.evaluate(mat,[0,0,1],[0,0,1],[0,0,1]);
  if(brdf.some(x=>!Number.isFinite(x)||x<0))errors.push('Physical BRDF finite/positive test failed');

  const metallic=new RenderSurfaceMaterial({
    id:'m',name:'m',shading:'physical',baseColor:[.9,.7,.2],
    roughness:.25,metalness:1,opacity:1,emissive:[0,0,0],emissiveIntensity:0
  });
  const mb=PhysicalBSDF.evaluate(metallic,[0,0,1],[0,0,1],[0,0,1]);
  if(mb.some(x=>!Number.isFinite(x)||x<0))errors.push('Metallic BRDF test failed');

  const tm=RenderColor.aces([10,3,1]);
  if(tm.some(x=>x<0||x>1||!Number.isFinite(x)))errors.push('Tone mapping range test failed');

  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}
window.runRenderGroup5SelfTests=runRenderGroup5SelfTests;

function validateRenderGroup5(){
  const errors=[];
  if(typeof RenderSurfaceMaterial!=='function')errors.push('RenderSurfaceMaterial missing');
  if(typeof PhysicalBSDF.evaluate!=='function')errors.push('PhysicalBSDF missing');
  if(typeof BasicDirectRenderer.render!=='function')errors.push('BasicDirectRenderer missing');
  if(typeof SurfaceInterpolator.normal!=='function')errors.push('SurfaceInterpolator missing');
  const tests=runRenderGroup5SelfTests();if(!tests.ok)errors.push(...tests.errors);
  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}

  rectangle:Object.freeze({intensity:1,size:25,width:50,height:50,range:0,coneAngle:90,penumbra:0}),
  disc:Object.freeze({intensity:1,size:25,range:0,coneAngle:90,penumbra:0}),
  sphere:Object.freeze({intensity:1,size:25,range:0,coneAngle:180,penumbra:0}),
  sun:Object.freeze({intensity:1,size:.5,range:0,coneAngle:.53,penumbra:0}),

// v3.98.3: one artistic intensity scale for every light type.
// Intensity=1 is the common baseline. Local lights use a gentle scene-unit falloff
// so they remain useful instead of becoming nearly black from raw 1/r^2 at cm-scale distances.
const LightEnergyPolicy=Object.freeze({
  referenceDistance:100,
  distanceAttenuation(light,distance){
    const d=Math.max(0,Number(distance)||0);
    const ref=this.referenceDistance;
    let a=1/(1+(d/ref)*(d/ref));
    const range=Math.max(0,Number(light?.range)||0);
    if(range>0){
      if(d>=range)return 0;
      const t=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,1-d/range));
      // smooth finite-range fade without changing the shared intensity unit.
      a*=t*t*(3-2*t);
    }
    return a;
  },
  radiance(light,factor=1){
    const intensity=Math.max(0,Number(light?.intensity)||0);
    return RenderColor.mul(light.color,intensity*Math.max(0,Number(factor)||0));
  }
});
window.LightEnergyPolicy=LightEnergyPolicy;
window.__3DLiteLightEnergy3983=true;
window.__3DLiteLightDefaultDirection3983=true;
window.ThreeDLiteLightEnergy3983=Object.freeze({version:'3.99.0',baselineIntensity:1,referenceDistance:100});

const RenderCameraRayGenerator=Object.freeze({
  rotateByQuaternion(v,q){
    const x=v[0],y=v[1],z=v[2],qx=q[0],qy=q[1],qz=q[2],qw=q[3];
    const ix=qw*x+qy*z-qz*y,iy=qw*y+qz*x-qx*z,iz=qw*z+qx*y-qy*x,iw=-qx*x-qy*y-qz*z;
    return [
      ix*qw+iw*-qx+iy*-qz-iz*-qy,
      iy*qw+iw*-qy+iz*-qx-ix*-qz,
      iz*qw+iw*-qz+ix*-qy-iy*-qx
    ];
  },
  ray(cameraSnapshot,px,py,width,height){
    const c=RenderCameraPolicy.sanitize(cameraSnapshot);
    width=Math.max(1,Number(width)||1);height=Math.max(1,Number(height)||1);
    const nx=((px+.5)/width)*2-1;
    const ny=1-((py+.5)/height)*2;
    const aspect=Math.max(1e-6,width/height);
    const fovRad=c.fov*Math.PI/180,tan=Math.tan(fovRad*.5);
    const local=RayMath.normalize([nx*aspect*tan,ny*tan,-1]);
    const world=RayMath.normalize(this.rotateByQuaternion(local,c.quaternion));
    return new RenderRay(c.position,world,c.near,c.far,RenderRayType.CAMERA,0);
  }
});

var RenderAccelerationSet = class RenderAccelerationSet{
  constructor(store,bvh){
    this.store=store;this.bvh=bvh;
    this.vertexCount=store.positions.length/3;
    this.triangleCount=store.triangleCount();
    this.nodeCount=bvh.nodes.length;
    this.stats=Object.freeze({geometry:store.stats,bvh:bvh.stats()});
    Object.freeze(this);
  }
  trace(ray){return this.bvh.intersect(ray)}
  occluded(ray){return this.bvh.anyHit(ray)}
}


const RenderAccelerationCache16={
  geometryRevision:null,
  acceleration:null,
  hits:0,
  misses:0,
  reset(){this.geometryRevision=null;this.acceleration=null;this.hits=0;this.misses=0;},
  invalidate(){this.geometryRevision=null;this.acceleration=null;},
  revision(scene){
    const m=scene?.metadata||{};
    return [m.sceneRevision??'s?',m.geometryRevision??'g?',m.materialRevision??'m?',m.lightRevision??'l?',scene?.objects?.length||0,
      ...(scene?.objects||[]).map(o=>`${o.id}:${o.visible!==false?1:0}:${o.mesh?.positions?.length||0}:${o.mesh?.indices?.length||0}`)
    ].join('|');
  },
  get(scene){
    const rev=this.revision(scene);
    if(this.acceleration&&this.geometryRevision===rev){this.hits++;return this.acceleration;}
    this.misses++;return null;
  },
  put(scene,acceleration){
    this.geometryRevision=this.revision(scene);
    this.acceleration=acceleration;
    return acceleration;
  },
  stats(){return Object.freeze({geometryRevision:this.geometryRevision,hits:this.hits,misses:this.misses,reused:!!this.acceleration});}
};

/*
  BLAS/TLAS boundary:
  - object mesh geometry revisions feed BLAS-equivalent cached acceleration data;
  - scene/object transform revisions are intentionally kept outside this cache key
    so a future TLAS can update instances without rebuilding object geometry.
  Current browser renderer still uses the proven binary RenderAccelerationSet.
*/
const RenderAccelerationHierarchy16=Object.freeze({
  mode:'geometry-revision acceleration cache; TLAS/BLAS boundary reserved',
  cache:RenderAccelerationCache16,
  build(scene,builder){
    const cached=RenderAccelerationCache16.get(scene);
    if(cached)return cached;
    return RenderAccelerationCache16.put(scene,builder());
  }
});
window.RenderAccelerationCache16=RenderAccelerationCache16;
window.RenderAccelerationHierarchy16=RenderAccelerationHierarchy16;

const RenderAccelerationBuilder=Object.freeze({
  build(renderScene){
    return RenderAccelerationHierarchy16.build(renderScene,()=>{
      const packets=RenderGeometryCompiler.compileScene(renderScene);
      const store=new RenderTriangleStore();
      packets.forEach((p,i)=>store.addPacket(p,i));
      store.finalize();
      return new RenderAccelerationSet(store,new RenderBVH(store,4));
    });
  }
});

const DiagnosticRenderer=Object.freeze({
  async render(job,acceleration,onProgress=()=>{}){
    const width=Math.min(640,Math.max(1,job.progress.resolution[0]));
    const height=Math.min(360,Math.max(1,job.progress.resolution[1]));
    const rgba=new Uint8ClampedArray(width*height*4);
    const cameraSnapshot=job.renderScene.camera;
    const rowChunk=8;
    for(let y=0;y<height;y++){
      if(job.cancelled||job.controlToken16?.cancelled?.())throw Object.assign(new Error('Render job cancelled'),{cancelled:true});
      while((job.paused||job.controlToken16?.paused?.())&&!job.cancelled)await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,20));
      for(let x=0;x<width;x++){
        const ray=RenderCameraRayGenerator.ray(cameraSnapshot,x,y,width,height);
        perf16.addRay(RenderRayType.CAMERA);
        perf16.addRay(RenderRayType.CAMERA);
        const hit=acceleration.trace(ray);
        const o=(y*width+x)*4;
        if(hit.hit){
          const n=hit.geometricNormal;
          rgba[o]=Math.round((n[0]*.5+.5)*255);
          rgba[o+1]=Math.round((n[1]*.5+.5)*255);
          rgba[o+2]=Math.round((n[2]*.5+.5)*255);
          rgba[o+3]=255;
        }else{
          rgba[o]=24;rgba[o+1]=24;rgba[o+2]=24;rgba[o+3]=255;
        }
      }
      if(y%rowChunk===0)onProgress((y+1)/height);
      if(y%rowChunk===0)await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,0));
    }
    return {width,height,rgba};
  }
});

      if(job.cancelled||job.controlToken16?.cancelled?.())throw Object.assign(new Error('Render job cancelled'),{cancelled:true});
      while((job.paused||job.controlToken16?.paused?.())&&!job.cancelled)await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,20));
      for(let x=0;x<width;x++){
        const ray=RenderCameraRayGenerator.ray(cameraSnapshot,x,y,width,height);
        perf16.addRay(RenderRayType.CAMERA);
        perf16.addRay(RenderRayType.CAMERA);
        const hit=acceleration.trace(ray);
        const o=(y*width+x)*4;
        if(hit.hit){
          const n=hit.geometricNormal;
          rgba[o]=Math.round((n[0]*.5+.5)*255);
          rgba[o+1]=Math.round((n[1]*.5+.5)*255);
          rgba[o+2]=Math.round((n[2]*.5+.5)*255);
          rgba[o+3]=255;
        }else{
          rgba[o]=24;rgba[o+1]=24;rgba[o+2]=24;rgba[o+3]=255;
        }
      }
      if(y%rowChunk===0)onProgress((y+1)/height);
      if(y%rowChunk===0)await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,0));
    }
    return {width,height,rgba};
  }
});

});

const RenderCameraRayGenerator=Object.freeze({
  rotateByQuaternion(v,q){
    const x=v[0],y=v[1],z=v[2],qx=q[0],qy=q[1],qz=q[2],qw=q[3];
    const ix=qw*x+qy*z-qz*y,iy=qw*y+qz*x-qx*z,iz=qw*z+qx*y-qy*x,iw=-qx*x-qy*y-qz*z;
    return [
      ix*qw+iw*-qx+iy*-qz-iz*-qy,
      iy*qw+iw*-qy+iz*-qx-ix*-qz,
      iz*qw+iw*-qz+ix*-qy-iy*-qx
    ];
  },
  ray(cameraSnapshot,px,py,width,height){
    const c=RenderCameraPolicy.sanitize(cameraSnapshot);
    width=Math.max(1,Number(width)||1);height=Math.max(1,Number(height)||1);
    const nx=((px+.5)/width)*2-1;
    const ny=1-((py+.5)/height)*2;
    const aspect=Math.max(1e-6,width/height);
    const fovRad=c.fov*Math.PI/180,tan=Math.tan(fovRad*.5);
    const local=RayMath.normalize([nx*aspect*tan,ny*tan,-1]);
    const world=RayMath.normalize(this.rotateByQuaternion(local,c.quaternion));
    return new RenderRay(c.position,world,c.near,c.far,RenderRayType.CAMERA,0);
  }
});

var RenderAccelerationSet = class RenderAccelerationSet{
  constructor(store,bvh){
    this.store=store;this.bvh=bvh;
    this.vertexCount=store.positions.length/3;
    this.triangleCount=store.triangleCount();
    this.nodeCount=bvh.nodes.length;
    this.stats=Object.freeze({geometry:store.stats,bvh:bvh.stats()});
    Object.freeze(this);
  }
  trace(ray){return this.bvh.intersect(ray)}
  occluded(ray){return this.bvh.anyHit(ray)}
}


const RenderAccelerationCache16={
  geometryRevision:null,
  acceleration:null,
  hits:0,
  misses:0,
  reset(){this.geometryRevision=null;this.acceleration=null;this.hits=0;this.misses=0;},
  invalidate(){this.geometryRevision=null;this.acceleration=null;},
  revision(scene){
    const m=scene?.metadata||{};
    return [m.sceneRevision??'s?',m.geometryRevision??'g?',m.materialRevision??'m?',m.lightRevision??'l?',scene?.objects?.length||0,
      ...(scene?.objects||[]).map(o=>`${o.id}:${o.visible!==false?1:0}:${o.mesh?.positions?.length||0}:${o.mesh?.indices?.length||0}`)
    ].join('|');
  },
  get(scene){
    const rev=this.revision(scene);
    if(this.acceleration&&this.geometryRevision===rev){this.hits++;return this.acceleration;}
    this.misses++;return null;
  },
  put(scene,acceleration){
    this.geometryRevision=this.revision(scene);
    this.acceleration=acceleration;
    return acceleration;
  },
  stats(){return Object.freeze({geometryRevision:this.geometryRevision,hits:this.hits,misses:this.misses,reused:!!this.acceleration});}
};

/*
  BLAS/TLAS boundary:
  - object mesh geometry revisions feed BLAS-equivalent cached acceleration data;
  - scene/object transform revisions are intentionally kept outside this cache key
    so a future TLAS can update instances without rebuilding object geometry.
  Current browser renderer still uses the proven binary RenderAccelerationSet.
*/
const RenderAccelerationHierarchy16=Object.freeze({
  mode:'geometry-revision acceleration cache; TLAS/BLAS boundary reserved',
  cache:RenderAccelerationCache16,
  build(scene,builder){
    const cached=RenderAccelerationCache16.get(scene);
    if(cached)return cached;
    return RenderAccelerationCache16.put(scene,builder());
  }
});
window.RenderAccelerationCache16=RenderAccelerationCache16;
window.RenderAccelerationHierarchy16=RenderAccelerationHierarchy16;

const RenderAccelerationBuilder=Object.freeze({
  build(renderScene){
    return RenderAccelerationHierarchy16.build(renderScene,()=>{
      const packets=RenderGeometryCompiler.compileScene(renderScene);
      const store=new RenderTriangleStore();
      packets.forEach((p,i)=>store.addPacket(p,i));
      store.finalize();
      return new RenderAccelerationSet(store,new RenderBVH(store,4));
    });
  }
});

const DiagnosticRenderer=Object.freeze({
  async render(job,acceleration,onProgress=()=>{}){
    const width=Math.min(640,Math.max(1,job.progress.resolution[0]));
    const height=Math.min(360,Math.max(1,job.progress.resolution[1]));
    const rgba=new Uint8ClampedArray(width*height*4);
    const cameraSnapshot=job.renderScene.camera;
    const rowChunk=8;
    for(let y=0;y<height;y++){
      if(job.cancelled||job.controlToken16?.cancelled?.())throw Object.assign(new Error('Render job cancelled'),{cancelled:true});
      while((job.paused||job.controlToken16?.paused?.())&&!job.cancelled)await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,20));
      for(let x=0;x<width;x++){
        const ray=RenderCameraRayGenerator.ray(cameraSnapshot,x,y,width,height);
        perf16.addRay(RenderRayType.CAMERA);
        perf16.addRay(RenderRayType.CAMERA);
        const hit=acceleration.trace(ray);
        const o=(y*width+x)*4;
        if(hit.hit){
          const n=hit.geometricNormal;
          rgba[o]=Math.round((n[0]*.5+.5)*255);
          rgba[o+1]=Math.round((n[1]*.5+.5)*255);
          rgba[o+2]=Math.round((n[2]*.5+.5)*255);
          rgba[o+3]=255;
        }else{
          rgba[o]=24;rgba[o+1]=24;rgba[o+2]=24;rgba[o+3]=255;
        }
      }
      if(y%rowChunk===0)onProgress((y+1)/height);
      if(y%rowChunk===0)await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,0));
    }
    return {width,height,rgba};
  }
});

   const b=this.fromDirection(light.direction);
    const du=(u-.5)*light.width,dv=(v-.5)*light.height;

  pointOnDisc(light,u,v){
    const b=this.fromDirection(light.direction);
    const d=RenderSampling.concentricDisk(u,v);
    const r=light.size*.5;
    return RayMath.add(light.position,RayMath.add(RayMath.mul(b.tangent,d[0]*r),RayMath.mul(b.bitangent,d[1]*r)));
  },
  pointOnSphere(light,u,v){
    const z=1-2*u;
    const r=Math.sqrt(Math.max(0,1-z*z));
    const phi=2*Math.PI*v;
    const local=[r*Math.cos(phi),r*Math.sin(phi),z];
    return RayMath.add(light.position,RayMath.mul(local,light.size*.5));
  }
});

const EnvironmentSampler=Object.freeze({
  findDome(lights){return (lights||[]).find(l=>l.enabled!==false&&l.type==='dome')||null;},
  radiance(light,direction){
    if(!light||light.enabled===false||light.type!=='dome')return [0,0,0];
    return LightEnergyPolicy.radiance(light);
  },
  background(renderScene,settings,lights,rayDirection){
    const dome=this.findDome(lights);
    if(dome)return this.radiance(dome,rayDirection);
    return RenderColor.hexToLinear(settings.background||'#202020');
  }
});

const ShadowVisibility=Object.freeze({
  visible(acceleration,origin,direction,maxDistance){
    try{LiteTraceExecutionContext.performance?.addRay?.(RenderRayType.SHADOW);}catch(_){}
    const eps=RenderRayPolicy.epsilonForPoint(origin);
    if(Number.isFinite(maxDistance)&&maxDistance<=eps*2)return true;
    const o=RayMath.add(origin,RayMath.mul(direction,eps));
    const limit=Number.isFinite(maxDistance)?Math.max(eps,maxDistance-eps):Infinity;
    const ray=new RenderRay(o,direction,eps,limit,RenderRayType.SHADOW,0);
    return !acceleration.occluded(ray);
  }
});

const AreaLightSampler=Object.freeze({
  sample(light,point,u=.5,v=.5){
    if(light.type==='rectangle'){
      const lp=LightBasis.pointOnRectangle(light,u,v);
      return this._local(light,point,lp,RenderLightPolicy.area(light),light.direction);
    }
    if(light.type==='disc'){
      const lp=LightBasis.pointOnDisc(light,u,v);
      return this._local(light,point,lp,RenderLightPolicy.area(light),light.direction);
    }
    if(light.type==='sphere'){
      const lp=LightBasis.pointOnSphere(light,u,v);
      const normal=RayMath.normalize(RayMath.sub(lp,light.position));
      return this._local(light,point,lp,RenderLightPolicy.area(light),normal);
    }
    if(light.type==='mesh'){
      // Part 4 keeps Mesh Light as a rectangular proxy.
      // True emissive mesh-triangle sampling remains Group 10.
      const lp=LightBasis.pointOnRectangle(light,u,v);
      return this._local(light,point,lp,RenderLightPolicy.area(light),light.direction);
    }
    return DirectLightSampler.sample(light,point);
  },

  _local(light,point,lightPoint,area,emitterNormal=null){
    const to=RayMath.sub(lightPoint,point);
    const dist=Math.max(RayMath.length(to),1e-6);
    const dir=RayMath.mul(to,1/dist);

    if(light.range>0&&dist>=light.range){
      return {kind:'local',direction:dir,distance:dist,radiance:[0,0,0],lightPoint,area};
    }

    let attenuation=LightEnergyPolicy.distanceAttenuation(light,dist);

    let emitterCos=1;
    if(emitterNormal){
      emitterCos=Math.max(0,RayMath.dot(RayMath.normalize(emitterNormal),RayMath.mul(dir,-1)));
      if(emitterCos<=0){
        return {kind:'local',direction:dir,distance:dist,radiance:[0,0,0],lightPoint,area,emitterCos:0};
      }
    }

    const safeArea=Math.max(RenderLightPolicy.minArea,area);
    return {
      kind:'local',
      direction:dir,
      distance:dist,
      // Size changes sampling footprint/softness only; intensity remains the same user-facing unit.
      radiance:LightEnergyPolicy.radiance(light,attenuation*emitterCos),
      lightPoint,
      area:safeArea,
      emitterCos
    };
  }
});

var DirectLightingIntegrator=Object.freeze({
  samplesFor(light,settings){
    if(['rectangle','disc','sphere','mesh'].includes(light.type)){
      const preset=String(settings?.quality||'Preview');
      if(preset==='Ultra')return 16;
      if(preset==='High')return 8;
      if(preset==='Draft')return 1;
      return 4;
    }
    return 1;
  },

  evaluate(job,acceleration,compiled,ray,hit,pixelSeed=1,options={}){
    const material=RenderMaterialLibrary.resolveHit(job.renderScene,compiled.materials,hit);
    const n=SurfaceInterpolator.normal(acceleration,hit);
    const v=RayMath.mul(ray.direction,-1);
    const includeEmission=options.includeEmission!==false;
    const includeDome=options.includeDome!==false;
    let color=includeEmission?RenderColor.mul(material.emissive,material.emissiveIntensity):[0,0,0];

    if(material.shading==='unlit'){
      return includeEmission?RenderColor.add(material.baseColor,color):[0,0,0];
    }

    const emissiveSet=compiled.emissiveTriangles||job.emissiveTriangles||null;
    if(emissiveSet?.triangles?.length){
      const es=EmissiveTriangleSet.sample(emissiveSet,pixelSeed,0,hit.position);
      if(es&&ShadowVisibility.visible(acceleration,hit.position,es.direction,es.distance)){
        const brdf=PhysicalBSDF.evaluate(material,n,v,es.direction);
        const bsdfPdf=PathBSDFSampler.pdf(material,n,v,es.direction);
        const w=RenderMIS.power(es.pdf,bsdfPdf);
        color=RenderColor.add(color,RenderColor.mul(RenderColor.mul(brdf,es.radiance),w));
      }
    }

    for(let li=0;li<compiled.lights.length;li++){
      const light=compiled.lights[li];

      if(light.type==='dome'){
        if(includeDome){
          const ambient=EnvironmentSampler.radiance(light,n);
          const diffuse=RenderColor.mul(material.baseColor,(1-material.metalness)/Math.PI);
          color=RenderColor.add(color,RenderColor.mul(RenderColor.mul(diffuse,ambient),Math.PI));
        }
        continue;
      }

      const count=this.samplesFor(light,job.settings);
      let accum=[0,0,0];

      for(let si=0;si<count;si++){
        const uv=RenderSampling.sample2D(pixelSeed^(li*2654435761),si);
        const sample=['rectangle','disc','sphere','mesh'].includes(light.type)
          ? AreaLightSampler.sample(light,hit.position,uv[0],uv[1])
          : DirectLightSampler.sample(light,hit.position);

        if(!sample||!sample.direction)continue;
        if(!sample.radiance||sample.radiance.every(v=>v<=0||!Number.isFinite(v)))continue;

        if(light.castShadows!==false){
          const visible=ShadowVisibility.visible(acceleration,hit.position,sample.direction,sample.distance);
          if(!visible)continue;
        }

        const brdf=PhysicalBSDF.evaluate(material,n,v,sample.direction);
        accum=RenderColor.add(accum,RenderColor.mul(brdf,sample.radiance));
      }

      if(count>1)accum=RenderColor.mul(accum,1/count);
      color=RenderColor.add(color,accum);
    }

    return color;
  }
});

const RenderAccelerationBuilder=Object.freeze({
  build(renderScene){
    return RenderAccelerationHierarchy16.build(renderScene,()=>{
      const packets=RenderGeometryCompiler.compileScene(renderScene);
      const store=new RenderTriangleStore();
      packets.forEach((p,i)=>store.addPacket(p,i));
      store.finalize();
      return new RenderAccelerationSet(store,new RenderBVH(store,4));
    });
  }
});

  async render(job,acceleration,onProgress=()=>{}){
    const width=Math.min(640,Math.max(1,job.progress.resolution[0]));
    const height=Math.min(360,Math.max(1,job.progress.resolution[1]));

      for(let x=0;x<width;x++){
        const ray=RenderCameraRayGenerator.ray(cameraSnapshot,x,y,width,height);
        perf16.addRay(RenderRayType.CAMERA);
        perf16.addRay(RenderRayType.CAMERA);
        const hit=acceleration.trace(ray);
        const o=(y*width+x)*4;
        if(hit.hit){
          const n=hit.geometricNormal;
          rgba[o]=Math.round((n[0]*.5+.5)*255);
          rgba[o+1]=Math.round((n[1]*.5+.5)*255);
          rgba[o+2]=Math.round((n[2]*.5+.5)*255);
          rgba[o+3]=255;
        }else{
          rgba[o]=24;rgba[o+1]=24;rgba[o+2]=24;rgba[o+3]=255;
        }
      }
      if(y%rowChunk===0)onProgress((y+1)/height);
      if(y%rowChunk===0)await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,0));
    }
    return {width,height,rgba};
  }
});

  evaluateDirect(job,acceleration,compiled,ray,hit,pixelSeed=1,options={}){

  },

    if(typeof this.evaluateDirect!=='function')errors.push('Core C direct evaluator missing');
    return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};

  estimate(job,acceleration,compiled,hit,n,material,seed,bounce){
    const cache=job.lightCache;if(!cache||!this.cacheable(material))return null;
    const found=cache.query(hit.position,n);
    if(found){
      const cadence=LightCachePolicy.retraceEvery(job);
      if(cadence>0&&cache.hits%cadence===0){
        cache.retraces++;
        const fresh=this.probe(job,acceleration,compiled,hit,n,material,seed^0xB5297A4D,bounce);
        cache.refreshNearest(hit.position,n,fresh);
        return RenderColor.mul(RenderColor.add(RenderColor.mul(found,.75),RenderColor.mul(fresh,.25)),1);
      }
      return found;
    }

    const outgoing=this.probe(job,acceleration,compiled,hit,n,material,seed,bounce);
    cache.insert(hit.position,n,outgoing);
    return outgoing;
  }

var PathIntegrator=Object.freeze({
  maxDepth(job){
    return Math.max(1,Math.min(16,Number(job?.settings?.maxBounces)||5));
  },

  finiteColor(c){
    return Array.isArray(c)&&c.length===3&&c.every(v=>Number.isFinite(v)&&v>=0);
  },

  safeColor(c){
    return this.finiteColor(c)?c:[0,0,0];
  },

  offsetOrigin(hit,n,direction){
    const sign=RayMath.dot(direction,n)>=0?1:-1;
    const eps=RenderRayPolicy.epsilonForPoint(hit.position);
    return RayMath.add(hit.position,RayMath.mul(n,eps*sign));
  },

  trace(job,acceleration,compiled,primaryRay,pixelSeed,initialHit=null){
    let radiance=[0,0,0];
    let throughput=[1,1,1];
    let ray=primaryRay;
    let previousGuide=null;
    const maxDepth=this.maxDepth(job);
    const maxPathRays=Math.max(1,Math.min(4096,Number(job?.settings?.maxPathRays)||64));
    let pathRays=1;

    for(let bounce=0;bounce<maxDepth;bounce++){
      const hit=(bounce===0&&initialHit)?initialHit:acceleration.trace(ray);

      if(!hit.hit){
        const env=this.safeColor(RenderCoreD.evaluateEnvironment(job,compiled,ray.direction));
        radiance=RenderColor.add(radiance,RenderColor.mul(throughput,env));
        if(previousGuide&&job.pathGuide){
          const w=Math.max(0,.2126*env[0]+.7152*env[1]+.0722*env[2]);
          job.pathGuide.record(previousGuide.position,previousGuide.normal,previousGuide.direction,w);
        }
        break;
      }

      const material=RenderMaterialLibrary.resolveHit(job.renderScene,compiled.materials,hit);
      const n=SurfaceInterpolator.normal(acceleration,hit);
      const v=RayMath.mul(ray.direction,-1);

      // Next-event direct lighting at each hit. Dome is excluded here because
      // environment energy is accounted for by path miss evaluation.
      const direct=Group9DirectMIS.evaluate(
        job,acceleration,compiled,ray,hit,pixelSeed^(bounce*0x9E3779B9)
      );
      const safeDirect=this.safeColor(direct);
      radiance=RenderColor.add(radiance,RenderColor.mul(throughput,safeDirect));

      if(previousGuide&&job.pathGuide){
        const w=Math.max(0,.2126*safeDirect[0]+.7152*safeDirect[1]+.0722*safeDirect[2]);
        job.pathGuide.record(previousGuide.position,previousGuide.normal,previousGuide.direction,w);
      }

      if(bounce>=1){
        if(job?.settings?.secondaryGI==='None')break;
        if(LightCacheEstimator.enabled(job)){
          const cachedTail=LightCacheEstimator.estimate(job,acceleration,compiled,hit,n,material,pixelSeed,bounce);
          if(cachedTail){
            radiance=RenderColor.add(radiance,RenderColor.mul(throughput,cachedTail));
            break;
          }
        }
      }

      if(material.shading==='unlit')break;

      const sample=GuidedBSDFSampler.sample(job,material,n,v,hit.position,pixelSeed,bounce);
      if(!sample)break;

      throughput=RenderColor.mul(throughput,sample.weight);

      if(!this.finiteColor(throughput))break;
      if(Math.max(throughput[0],throughput[1],throughput[2])<1e-6)break;

      // Russian roulette after the first few bounces. Survival probability tracks path throughput.
      if(bounce>=2){
        const survive=Math.max(.08,Math.min(.95,Math.max(throughput[0],throughput[1],throughput[2])));
        if(PathSampler.scalar(pixelSeed,bounce,15)>survive)break;
        throughput=RenderColor.mul(throughput,1/survive);
      }

      if(sample.transmission&&material.thickness>0){
        throughput=RenderColor.mul(
          throughput,
          DielectricTransport.beerLambert(material.attenuationColor,material.thickness,material.attenuationDistance)
        );
      }

      previousGuide=(!sample.transmission&&job.pathGuide&&RayMath.dot(n,sample.direction)>0)
        ? {position:hit.position.slice(),normal:n.slice(),direction:sample.direction.slice()}
        : null;

      if(pathRays>=maxPathRays)break;
      pathRays++;
      const origin=this.offsetOrigin(hit,n,sample.direction);
      const eps=RenderRayPolicy.epsilonForPoint(origin);
      const rayType=sample.transmission?RenderRayType.TRANSMISSION:(sample.lobe==='ggx'?RenderRayType.GLOSSY:RenderRayType.DIFFUSE);
      RenderCoreH.attachPerformance(job).addRay(rayType);
      ray=new RenderRay(origin,sample.direction,eps,Infinity,rayType,bounce+1);
    }

    return radiance;
  },

  tracePixel(job,acceleration,compiled,primaryRay,pixelSeed){
    const primaryHitRecord=acceleration.trace(primaryRay);
    if(!primaryHitRecord.hit){
      const env=this.safeColor(RenderCoreD.evaluateEnvironment(job,compiled,primaryRay.direction));
      return {radiance:env,primaryHit:false,primaryHitRecord:null};
    }
    return {
      radiance:this.trace(job,acceleration,compiled,primaryRay,pixelSeed,primaryHitRecord),
      primaryHit:true,
      primaryHitRecord
    };
  }
});

class ProgressiveAccumulator{
  constructor(width,height){
    this.width=width;this.height=height;
    const n=width*height;
    this.sum=new Float64Array(n*3);
    this.mean=new Float64Array(n);
    this.m2=new Float64Array(n);
    this.samples=new Uint32Array(n);
    this.converged=new Uint8Array(n);
    this.active=n;
  }
  add(index,color){
    const s=++this.samples[index];
    const o=index*3;
    this.sum[o]+=color[0];this.sum[o+1]+=color[1];this.sum[o+2]+=color[2];
    const lum=.2126*color[0]+.7152*color[1]+.0722*color[2];
    const delta=lum-this.mean[index];
    this.mean[index]+=delta/s;
    this.m2[index]+=delta*(lum-this.mean[index]);
  }
  variance(index){
    const s=this.samples[index];
    return s>1?Math.max(0,this.m2[index]/(s-1)):Infinity;
  }
  noise(index){
    const mean=Math.max(1e-6,Math.abs(this.mean[index]));
    const s=Math.max(1,this.samples[index]);
    return Math.sqrt(this.variance(index)/s)/mean;
  }
  shouldSample(index,settings){
    if(!settings.adaptive)return this.samples[index]<settings.maxSamples;
    if(this.samples[index]<settings.minSamples)return true;
    if(this.samples[index]>=settings.maxSamples)return false;
    return this.noise(index)>settings.noiseThreshold;
  }
  markConverged(index){
    if(this.converged[index])return;
    this.converged[index]=1;this.active=Math.max(0,this.active-1);
